### [1] [FRIDA: Finding Rotation In ultra-Diffuse gAlaxies. I. Programme overview, resolved gas density profiles, and HI mass-size relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14635)
*Pavel E. Mancera Piña,Tom Oosterloo,Filippo Fraternali,Elizabeth A. K. Adams,Andrea Afruni,Cecilia Bacchini,Enrico M. Di Teodoro,Lexi Gault,Anastasia A. Ponomareva,Justin I. Read,Barbara Šiljeg*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: FRIDA项目利用MeerKAT和VLA对12个富气超弥散星系(UDG)进行高分辨率HI观测，发现其HI分布延展且规则，面密度剖面内平外指数下降，尺度与质量均比同质量普通星系更大更弥散，剖面形态介于晚型盘星系与早型星系之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富气超弥散星系（UDG）可在极低表面密度条件下检验星系形成模型，但其中性氢（HI）结构约束不足，限制了对该类星系族群的理解。

Method: 介绍FRIDA项目（MeerKAT与VLA联合观测12个富气UDG的高分辨率HI数据）的样本、观测与数据处理；测量HI流量、HI质量M_HI与分辨的面密度剖面Σ_HI(R)；通过HI形态建模估计倾角；将样本置于M_HI–R_HI与M_HI–R_50,HI质量-尺度关系及归一化剖面Σ_HI(R/R_HI)、质量累积分数M_HI(<R)/M_HI中与其他星系类型比较。

Result: UDG普遍具有延展（半径约7–30 kpc）且规则的HI发射，并发现其中一个UDG的卫星星系；多数UDG的Σ_HI(R)内区平坦（中心附近约4 M_⊙ pc^-2）、外区指数下降；中位倾角43°，与UDG选择效应下的盘星系理论预期一致；样本落在质量-尺度关系弥散范围内但系统性偏上侧，比同M_HI平均星系延展约15–25%、平均Σ_HI低约25–35%；归一化剖面介于晚型盘星系与早型星系之间，与Sa星系大量重叠。

Conclusion: 富气UDG具有规则延展的HI盘结构，其尺度偏大、表面密度偏低，HI剖面特征介于盘星系与早型星系之间，与Sa星系类似，为低表面密度下的星系形成模型提供了观测约束。

Abstract: Gas-rich ultra-diffuse galaxies (UDGs) provide powerful tests of galaxy formation models at low surface densities. However, the structure of their neutral atomic hydrogen (HI) remains poorly constrained, hampering our understanding of this population.
  We present FRIDA (Finding Rotation In ultra-Diffuse gAlaxies), a MeerKAT and VLA programme providing high-resolution HI data of 12 gas-rich UDGs. In this paper, we introduce our sample and delve into the associated observations and data reduction. We derive HI fluxes, masses ($M_{\rm HI}$), and resolved surface density profiles ($Σ_{\rm HI}(R)$). We find extended ($\sim 7-30\ \rm{kpc}$ in radius) and regular HI emission in our UDGs, and we report the discovery of a satellite around one of them. Most UDGs in FRIDA have $Σ_{\rm HI}(R)$ profiles with flat inner parts reaching $Σ_{\rm HI}(R)\approx 4\ M_\odot\ \rm{pc^{-2}}$ near their centres, which then decline exponentially in their outer parts. By modelling the HI morphology of the UDGs, we find a median inclination of $43^\circ$, in agreement with theoretical expectations for disc galaxies under UDG selection effects. Moreover, we place our UDGs in the $M_{\rm HI}-R_{\rm HI}$ and $M_{\rm HI}-R_{\rm 50,HI}$ mass-size relations, where $R_{\rm HI}$ is radius at which $Σ_{\rm HI}(R)=1\ M_\odot \rm{pc^{-2}}$, and $R_{\rm 50,HI}$ is the radius enclosing $50\%$ of $M_{\rm HI}$. While our UDGs lie within the scatter of the relations, they systematically fall on their upper side, being slightly (by $\sim 15-25\%$) more extended and of lower mean $Σ_{\rm HI}$ (by $\sim 25-35\%$) than average galaxies at similar $M_{\rm HI}$. Moreover, we find that $Σ_{\rm HI}(R/R_{\rm HI})$ and $M_{\rm HI}(<R)/M_{\rm HI}$ for UDGs follow an intermediate trend between late-type discs and early-type galaxies, with a large overlap with Sa galaxies.

</details>

TL;DR: 利用DESI光谱结合XGBoost的"配对法"变体直接测量恒星消光，并通过前向模型反演R(V)，发现高银纬尘埃区R(V)呈2.9与3.4的双峰分布（σ_NMAD=0.23）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消光曲线（以R(V)表征）随尘埃颗粒尺寸与成分变化，精确的R(V)对去红化天文颜色和理解星际介质至关重要；而常用的SFD方法基于热辐射，并非直接测量红化。

Method: 基于DESI光谱，采用机器学习化的"配对法"：用XGBoost由光谱参数（T_eff、log g、[Fe/H]）预测恒星内禀颜色，从观测颜色中扣除得到g、r、z、W1、W2波段组合的红化；再构建前向模型将E(g-r)与E(g-W1)映射到R(V)。

Result: g-r与r-z红化精度约0.02 mag；在DESI天区高银纬、E(g-r)>0.1的尘埃区，R(V)显著变化，σ_NMAD(R(V))=0.23，且呈双峰（峰值2.9和3.4），可能源于消光曲线形状的大尺度相关性与显著红化视线数目较少。R(V)与热辐射、尘埃温度、谱发射率指数β及Hα等示踪量无明显关系；但SFD预测消光的误差与R(V)高度相关。

Conclusion: 单条光学红化结合远红外尘埃亮度可预测消光曲线的变化，为改进SFD类消光图提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The dust extinction curve, characterized by the total-to-selective extinction ratio, $R(V)$, varies with grain size and composition. Accurate $R(V)$ values are crucial for dereddening astronomical colors and understanding the interstellar medium. We measure extinction toward stars with Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) spectroscopy using a machine-learning-based relative of the "pair method," matching reddened and unreddened stars. We use XGBoost to predict stars' intrinsic colors from their spectroscopic parameters ($T_\mathrm{eff}$, $\log g$, $[\mathrm{Fe/H}]$) and subtract them from observed colors to measure reddening in combinations of the $g$, $r$, $z$, $W1$, and $W2$ bands. This approach directly measures reddening, unlike the common thermal-emission-based method of Schlegel et al. (1998; SFD). The reddening is accurate to roughly 0.02 mag in $g-r$ and $r-z$. We develop a forward model to map $g-r$ and $g-W1$ reddenings to $R(V)$ and find significant variation in $R(V)$, with $σ_\mathrm{NMAD}(R(V)) = 0.23$, in dusty regions ($E(g-r) > 0.1$) at high Galactic latitudes observed by DESI. In this footprint, $R(V)$ is bimodal, with modes at 2.9 and 3.4, likely due to large-spatial-scale correlations in the shape of the extinction curve combined with the relatively small number of distinct sightlines with substantial reddening. We find no strong relationship between $R(V)$ and other tracers of the interstellar medium, such as thermal emission, dust temperature, spectral emissivity index $β$, and $Hα$. However, the error in the SFD-predicted extinction correlates well with $R(V)$, implying that a single optical reddening combined with the far-infrared dust brightness can predict variations in the extinction curve.

</details>

*Dennis Lee,Brandon Hensley,Tzu-Ching Chang,Olivier Doré,Matt Ashby,Shuang-Shuang Chen,Yun-Ting Cheng,Yi-Kuan Chiang,Brendan Crill,Ari Cukierman,Andreas Faisst,Joe Hora,Zhaoyu,Huai,Howard Hui,Woong-Seob Jeong,Miju Kang,Carey Lisse,Dan Masters,Gary Melnick,Chi Nguyen,Roberta Paladini,Ji Yeon Seok,Volker Tolls,Michael Werner,Mike Zemcov*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用SPHEREx对3.3 μm PAH发射特征的观测构建银河尘埃红化图，在北黄极22°×22°区域内测得E(B-V)图，与远红外、恒星和HI红化图在角分尺度一致（典型差异≲7 mmag），并可达约6''的角分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红化图（远红外、恒星、HI）角分辨率有限，需要验证SPHEREx的3.3 μm PAH发射特征能否作为高角分辨率红化示踪量，以服务于高银纬弥散区的精细消光改正。

Method: 对SPHEREx数据进行基于光谱模板的拟合，在北黄极中心22°×22°区域内以6''.2像素为单位测量3.3 μm PAH特征强度；再通过辐射场改正和线性比例因子将PAH强度转换为E(B-V)红化。采用与远红外、恒星、HI红化图对比及星系计数检验进行验证。

Result: 所得E(B-V)图与远红外、恒星和HI红化图在角分尺度一致，典型E(B-V)差异≲7 mmag；星系计数检验显示各红化图的弥散均约11 mmag。在高PAH丰度区存在红化被高估的迹象，但该效应小于辐射场改正不确定度，且在NEP最极端区域也仅约10 mmag。

Conclusion: 对于NEP这类高银纬弥散区及本文验证指标的灵敏度水平，基于SPHEREx PAH发射的红化图精度可媲美现有红化图，同时具备约6''的更高角分辨率，具备成为高分辨率红化示踪手段的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate the ability of SPHEREx observations of the $3.3$ $μ$m polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon (PAH) emission feature to trace Galactic dust reddening. Applying a spectral template-based method to SPHEREx data, we measure the strength of the $3.3$ $μ$m PAH feature in each $6''.2$ pixel of a $22^{\circ}$ by $22^{\circ}$ region centered on the north ecliptic pole (NEP). We convert the PAH feature strength to reddening by applying a radiation field correction and a linear scale factor. The resulting $E(B-V)$ map is consistent with far-infrared, stellar, and HI-based reddening maps at arcminute scales, exhibiting a typical $E(B-V)$ difference of $\lesssim 7$ mmag. A galaxy count test finds that all reddening maps have a dispersion of $\approx11$ mmag in $E(B-V)$ relative to what would be needed to produce spatially uniform galaxy counts. While there is some evidence that reddening is overestimated by our map at high PAH abundances, this effect is smaller than the uncertainties on the applied radiation field corrections and limited to $\sim$10 mmag in the most extreme regions of the NEP. For a diffuse, high Galactic latitude region like the NEP and at the sensitivities of our validation metrics, the SPHEREx PAH-emission based reddening map matches the accuracy of existing reddening maps while being capable of accessing a higher angular resolution of $\sim6''$.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用DIISC巡天的11个星系、542个Hα亮区，结合光学光谱与HI成像，识别出75个异常低金属丰度（ALM）区域，并推断其源于低金属丰度气体的局部吸积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究星系中异常低金属丰度恒星形成区的成因，检验局部贫金属气体吸积是否为驱动星系气体循环与维持恒星形成的机制。

Method: 结合MMT/Binospec对542个Hα亮区的光学光谱、VATT成像与VLA的HI 21cm成像；分别基于星系径向金属丰度梯度的偏离和分辨质量—金属丰度关系（rMZR）两种方法识别ALM区域，并估算吸积质量与氢吸积率。

Result: 共识别75个ALM区域（径向梯度法19个、rMZR法27个、两者兼有29个）；双方法一致区域比其银心距与局部恒星质量面密度预期低>0.1 dex；48个梯度法区域吸积质量5.6<log(M_acc/M☉)<7.5，与高速云质量相当；平均氢吸积率1.02 M☉/yr每星系，与平均恒星形成率相当；最强候选多位于R25内或附近，仅rMZR区域主导外区；沿单一视线的CGM Lyα吸收与盘化学结构无相关性证据。

Conclusion: 低金属丰度气体的局部吸积可维持星系恒星形成，ALM区域提供了追踪持续气体循环的空间分辨间接观测途径。

Abstract: We investigate anomalously low-metallicity (ALM) star-forming regions in 11 galaxies from the Deciphering the Interplay between the Interstellar Medium, Stars, and the Circumgalactic Medium (DIISC) survey. We combine optical spectroscopy of 542 H$α$-bright regions from MMT/Binospec with imaging from the Vatican Advanced Technology Telescope and H I 21 cm imaging from the Very Large Array. We find 75 ALM regions: 19 identified using deviations from each galaxy's radial metallicity gradient, 27 using the resolved mass--metallicity relation (rMZR), and 29 via both methods. Regions identified by both methods are metal-poor relative to their galactocentric positions and local stellar mass surface densities by $>$ 0.1 dex, making them the strongest candidates for localized metallicity dilution. We estimate accreted masses of $5.6<\log(M_{\rm acc}/{\rm M_\odot})<7.5$ for the 48 radial-gradient-selected regions, comparable to observed and simulated high-velocity cloud masses. We calculate a mean hydrogen accretion rate of $1.02\ {\rm M_\odot yr^{-1}}$ per galaxy, which is comparable to the mean star formation rate of the sample. The strongest ALM candidates lie within or near $R_{25}$, while rMZR-only regions dominate the outer population. We find no evidence that circumgalactic Ly$α$ absorption along a single sightline traces disk chemical structure, as quantified by either the radial metallicity gradient or the fraction of radial-gradient-selected ALM regions. These results suggest that localized accretion of low-metallicity gas can sustain star formation in galaxies and demonstrate that ALM regions provide a spatially resolved indirect view of ongoing gas cycling.

</details>


### [5] [La Silla-QUEST RR Lyrae Survey III: The Shape of the Outer Virgo Overdensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14734)
*R. Zinn,P. S. Coppi,C. Baltay,D. Rabinowitz,E. P. Bellinger,K. Bettwy*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用La Silla-QUEST与Gaia巡天在外维鲁戈超密度(OVOD)中证认256颗天琴座RR型变星，发现OVOD形态远大于且复杂于以往认知，可能起源于大质量矮星系，并可能是GSE并合产生的壳层结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新刻画OVOD的形态、距离与星族性质，以检验其是否为星流、其前身天体性质，以及是否与Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus(GSE)并合壳层假说相符。

Method: 结合La Silla-QUEST与Gaia巡天数据，在OVOD天区证认256颗RR Lyrae星(RRLS)；利用RRab周期统计（均值、Bailey图上的Oosterhoff分类、由周期推算金属丰度）与空间分布（长轴、宽度、弯曲度、北-南距离梯度），并与中央红巨星轨道作几何比对。

Result: OVOD平均距离约85 kpc，长轴约57°(约85 kpc)，宽度从约10°(约15 kpc)变化至显著鼓包处的约35°(约50 kpc)；相对大圆弯曲约35°，故不似星流。RRab周期跨度大，均值0.59天，仅19±3%接近Bailey图中Oosterhoff II的位置；平均[Fe/H]=-1.60，1σ弥散0.32。南延伸部与中央红巨星轨道成约70°夹角，支持GSE壳层假说；同时存在小的南北距离梯度。

Conclusion: 大量RRLS及其中低金属丰度暗示OVOD前身为大质量矮星系；其非星流的复杂弯曲形态支持GSE并合壳层解释，但形态与距离梯度也可能反映在天空上投影叠加的复合结构，需进一步区分。

Abstract: Using the La Silla-QUEST and Gaia surveys, we identify 256 RR Lyrae stars (RRLS) in the Outer Virgo Overdensity (OVOD). These show that the OVOD has a signifcantly larger and more complex shape than previously recognized. The OVOD lies at an average distance of $\sim 85$ kpc, has a long dimension of $\sim 57^\circ$ ($\sim 85$ kpc), and a width that varies from $\sim 10^\circ $ ($\sim 15$ kpc) to $\sim 35^\circ $ ($\sim 50$ kpc) in a prominent bulge. Because it bends by $\sim 35^\circ$ from a great circle, it does not appear to be a stellar stream. The type ab RRLS (RRab) span a large range in period, with a mean of $0.59$ days, and only $19\pm 3 \%$ lie near the locus of Oosterhoff II variables in the Bailey diagram. The overall large number of RRLS along with a mean RRab metallicity ([Fe/H]) of -1.60 and $1 σ$ dispersion of 0.32, as inferred from the RRab periods, suggest that the OVOD progenitor was a large dwarf galaxy. The hypothesis that the OVOD may be a shell produced by the Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus merger \citep{chandra2023} is supported by the $\sim 70^\circ$ angle that the southern extension of the OVOD makes with the orbits of red giants in its central part. The complex shape of the overall RRLS distribution, including a small north-south distance gradient, may also indicate, however, that we are seeing a blended, composite structure projected on the sky.

</details>


### [6] [How Intensity Threshold Shapes the Velocity Dispersion-Size Relation in Molecular Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14852)
*Yaru Qi,Xin Liu,Fujun Du*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用MWISP巡天中GDCluster识别的4916个13CO分子云，定量刻画强度阈值对速度弥散-尺度关系指数γ与归一化C的影响，发现两者随阈值单调变化并饱和，可将文献中0.38–0.7的γ分歧解释为同一条阈值依赖曲线上的不同位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子云速度弥散-尺度关系（σ_v = C(R/1pc)^γ）的幂指数在文献中从γ≈0.38到0.5乃至更高范围不一，归一化系数C也随之变化，但强度阈值对这一离散的贡献此前未被定量刻画。

Method: 基于MWISP巡天的13CO数据，使用GDCluster（Liu & Du 2025）识别出4916个分子云；将γ和C作为强度阈值T_th的函数进行拟合；并按旋臂对样本分组比较；另以DBSCAN识别的13CO与12CO样本作独立检验。

Result: γ随阈值单调上升，全样本饱和值γ_sat≈0.56，按旋臂分组可达≈0.62与0.70；归一化C(T_th)单调下降，饱和于C_sat≈0.35 km/s，对应维里近似下约46 M⊙/pc²的面密度。γ≈0.38（Larson 1981）与γ≈0.5（Solomon et al. 1987）分别对应低阈值（≈1.2 K）与近饱和阈值（2.6 K），与两项研究的13CO等效边界温度大致吻合。DBSCAN样本呈同样的单调趋势，仅曲线形状定量不同。

Conclusion: 历史上争议的γ值及文献中0.38–0.7的分布，可能主要反映同一条阈值依赖曲线上的不同位置与样本组成差异；使用Larson型关系评估湍流与引力作用时须考虑该阈值效应。

Abstract: The velocity dispersion-size relation of molecular clouds, $σ_v = C\,(R/1\,{\rm pc})^γ$, has been reported with a power-law index ranging from $γ\approx$ 0.38 to 0.5 or higher, and a correspondingly varying normalization coefficient $C$, but the role of the intensity threshold in producing this scatter has not been quantitatively characterized. Using 4916 $^{13}$CO clouds identified with GDCluster (Liu & Du 2025) in the Milky Way Imaging Scroll Painting (MWISP) survey, we derive $γ$ and $C$ as functions of the intensity threshold $T_{\rm th}$. The index $γ$ increases monotonically with threshold, reaching a saturation value $γ_{\rm sat} \approx$ 0.56 for the full sample; splitting the sample by spiral arm gives higher values, $γ_{\rm sat} \approx$ 0.62 and 0.70. The normalization $C(T_{\rm th})$ declines monotonically, saturating at $C_{\rm sat} \approx$ 0.35 km/s and implying a surface density of ~46 Msun/pc^2 under a virial approximation. On the $γ(T_{\rm th})$ curve, $γ\approx$ 0.38 (Larson 1981) and $γ\approx$ 0.5 (Solomon et al. 1987) correspond to a low threshold ($T_{\rm th} \approx$ 1.2 K) and a near-saturation one (2.6 K), respectively, roughly consistent with the $^{13}$CO-equivalent boundary temperatures of these two studies. These two historically debated values, together with the broader $γ\approx$ 0.38-0.7 range reported in the literature, may largely reflect different positions along this single threshold-dependent curve and differences in sample composition. This threshold effect should therefore be considered when using Larson-type relations to assess the roles of turbulence and gravity. As an independent check, DBSCAN-identified $^{13}$CO and $^{12}$CO samples show the same monotonic trend in $γ$, though the curve shape differs quantitatively.

</details>

Abstract: The optical continuum variability of quasars is often described by a damped random walk (DRW) model, with characteristic damping timescales $τ_{\rm DRW}$ of hundreds of days and amplitudes $σ_{\rm DRW}$ of $\mathcal{O}(0.1)$ magnitudes. This model has been widely used as a null hypothesis to assess the significance of periodic signals in quasar light curves in the search for supermassive black hole binaries, the significance of extreme quasar variability like tidal disruption events (TDEs) and changing-look (CL) quasars, or to model time lags in reverberation mapping. However, the DRW model is known to be incomplete, and an incorrect null hypothesis can lead to biased significance estimates. We investigate the impact of such null-hypothesis mis-specification on the detection of periodic signals in synthetic quasar light curves and introduce the generative model Nullasar, capable of learning quasar variability directly from time-series data. We demonstrate that Nullasar, trained with flow matching, can learn delicate statistical properties of stochastic light-curve variability without explicitly assuming any noise model. We further demonstrate that Nullasar can learn correlated distributions of noise parameters and generate new light-curve realizations from a range of deviations from the Gaussian DRW noise model, approximating parametric descriptions of stochastic processes. When used as a null hypothesis for detecting periodic signals in synthetic light curves, Nullasar performs comparably to fitting the correct DRW model to each light curve, and outperforms parametric fitting when the variability follows a more flexible AR(2) model. Finally, we identify practical limitations, including sensitivity to contamination of the training set by non-null signals, and show that projecting the conditioning vector of the model onto its leading principal components largely mitigates it.

Abstract: Diffuse interstellar bands (DIBs) are absorption features of uncertain origin observed in the optical and near-infrared spectra of stars and galaxies. DIB $\lambda5780$ is preferentially detected in regions of high extinction, near- or super-solar metallicity ($12+\log({\rm O/H})\gtrsim8.5$), and moderate specific star-formation rates ($-10.5\lesssim\log({\rm sSFR}/{\rm yr}^{-1})\lesssim-9$). By contrast, it falls below our detection threshold in metal-poor or strongly ionized environments. It is rarely detected in regions with little or no recent star formation, with the few detections in such environments occurring primarily in regions driven by composite ionization. Abstract: The central regions of the Magellanic Clouds are crucial for understanding galaxy interactions and calibrating the cosmic distance scale, yet they remain observationally challenging due to extreme stellar crowding. Utilising approximately 40 epochs per filter and PSF-based photometry, we derived high-quality light curves for 573 and 2090 DCEPs in the LMC and SMC, respectively. Fourier parameters calculated from the light curves indicate that pulsational models incorporating a mildly non-canonical mass-luminosity relation and a mixing-length parameter of alpha = 1.7 best reproduce the observations. Crucially, we discovered a striking line-of-sight spatial dichotomy within the young SMC DCEP population (t < 116 Myr), which is bifurcated into two distinct structures separated by ~4 kpc. This provides independent stellar evidence tracing a recent direct collision with the LMC. Finally, by unifying fundamental and first-overtone pulsators, we derived a highly constrained relative distance between the Clouds. Comparing this to detached eclipsing binary distances, our data suggest a moderate metallicity correction for the Leavitt Law with gamma ~ -0.2 mag/dex.

TL;DR: 该文用单区模型比较"连续"与"离散"超新星反馈，发现即使单位恒星质量的期望注入能量相同，离散反馈因首发超新星延迟且非线性耗气，会使低质量晕在z≥5时出现显著恒星质量超量（10^7 M⊙晕达135%），该差异源于事件离散性而非随机IMF抽样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数星系形成模型用按IMF平均的连续能量注入率近似超新星反馈。在低质量暗物质晕中，该近似可能失效：单次离散超新星即可将超过气体库全部气体驱离，连续平均会掩盖这一情形。作者希望定量考察两种反馈模式在z≥5时的差异及其物理来源。

Method: 构建单区（one-zone）模型，在z≥5下同时实现两种反馈：连续模型中超新星按IMF平均、能量在有限时间区间内均匀注入；离散模型中逐个超新星被分辨、能量瞬时注入。两者单位恒星形成质量的期望能量相同。分析反馈启动时间、单次事件可驱离的气体量（为当时储气量的1.5–2.7倍）、气体库耗尽的非线性响应，并检验固定超新星数目与时刻、以及将同一能量分配为更多更小事件的影响。

Conclusion: 仅匹配单位恒星质量的反馈能量，并不保证在"保留气体直至首次超新星"的低质量晕中获得等效反馈；连续IMF平均近似会系统性低估恒星质量，且误差随晕质量降低而急剧增大。反馈模型的离散性（能量注入的时间集中度与事件次数）本身是决定低质量晕演化的重要物理量。

Abstract: Many galaxy formation models represent supernova feedback by a continuous energy injection rate averaged over the stellar initial mass function (IMF). This approximation can fail in low-mass dark matter halos, where single, discrete supernovae can unbind more gas than the reservoir holds. We investigate this using a one-zone model that characterises continuous and discrete supernova feedback at redshifts $z\geq5$. Both feedback modes inject the same expected energy per unit stellar mass formed. In the discrete model, individual supernovae are resolved and energy is injected instantaneously; in the continuous model, supernovae are IMF-averaged and energy injection is spread over a finite time interval. Once stars form, a supernova can unbind 1.5-2.7 times the gas present. Continuous feedback begins removing gas after $\sim 4$ Myr, whereas the first discrete supernova occurs after $\sim 10$ Myr, which produces a stellar mass excess before feedback takes effect. The excess persists because the reservoir responds nonlinearly: an event removes at most the gas present, and star formation halts once it is empty. Fixing the number and timing of supernovae does not remove the stellar excess, which indicates that it is not caused by stochastic IMF sampling. Instead, it vanishes when the same energy is divided among more, smaller events. These differences become more pronounced at bluer wavelengths and for higher core dust densities, leading to variations in the fitted Sérsic index of up to ~25%. This occurs even for a spatially invariant SED and can lead to wrong conclusions about intrinsic stellar populations and related astrophysical questions, such as their formation histories or the mechanisms of inside-out star formation quenching. A full 3D approach is necessary to properly model attenuation effects, which, if not accounted for, can bias inferences made about stellar populations, their evolutionary histories, and spatial distributions.

After accounting for an additional non-circular kinematic component identified in the central HI spectra, we construct an extended rotation curve to constrain the dark matter distribution. The resulting rotation curve is poorly reproduced by a cuspy Navarro-Frenk-White (NFW) halo but well described by cored pseudo-isothermal (ISO) and Burkert halo models. Consistently, a fit with a generalized NFW model yields an inner density slope of $γ=0.36\pm0.12$, quantitatively supporting a shallow central density profile. The best-fit ISO model gives a dark matter core radius of $7.62\pm0.80$ kpc, approximately four times the stellar half-light radius and substantially larger than typically found in dwarf galaxies. We investigate the main systematic uncertainties and find that the resulting core radii remain large, all lying within 3$σ$ of the fiducial fit.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多星族引入的多尺度结构显著影响球状星团动力学演化，但双星族星团中MF斜率随半径的径向剖面及其随动力学年龄的演化尚缺乏系统研究；同时近期观测（如47 Tuc外围P2的MF更平、大质量团簇可能偏好bottom-light IMF）需要理论模拟检验。

Method: 采用MOCCA代码开展一组蒙特卡洛模拟，跟踪含P1与P2两个星族的球状星团长期演化，考虑两体弛豫、质量分层与潮汐剥离；拟合P1、P2及整体MF斜率的三维与二维投影径向剖面；考察局部MF斜率等于现今全局MF斜率与IMF斜率处的特征团心距离及其随动力学年龄的演化；并额外引入三参数bottom-light IMF以及P1与P2 IMF不同的模拟（P2在低质量端更平）。

Result: P1、P2及整体的径向MF斜率剖面均可由折线幂律良好描述，拐点略位于半质量半径内侧，部分源于多星族的 多尺度结构；在3D与2D投影坐标中识别并追踪了多处特征团心距离随动力学年龄的演化；初始更向心集中的P2导致更陡的径向梯度及外围更陡的MF斜率，并进而影响星团全局取值。

Conclusion: 多星族的多尺度结构是球状星团径向MF斜率剖面形状与演化的关键因素，P2的初始空间分布决定了梯度陡度并影响全局MF；bottom-light IMF与P2低质量端更平的IMF设定可解释大质量团簇及47 Tuc外围的观测趋势，为观测与理论对比提供框架。

Abstract: The dynamical evolution of globular clusters is significantly influenced by the multiscale structure introduced by the presence of multiple stellar populations. In this study, we investigate the long-term dynamical evolution of the stellar mass function (MF) within globular clusters hosting two stellar populations using a suite of Monte Carlo simulations run with the MOCCA code. Dynamical processes including two-body relaxation, mass segregation, and tidal stripping result in distinct radial profiles describing the variation with distance from the cluster's center of the stellar MF slope within the first population (P1), the second population (P2), and the cluster as a whole. We show that these radial profiles are well described by a broken power law with a knee located slightly interior to the half-mass radius, a feature partially driven by the multiscale structure of the multiple populations. In both 3D and 2D projected coordinates, we explore several characteristic clustercentric distances within the profile, including those where the local MF slope matches the global value of the present-day MF slope and the initial mass function (IMF) slope, and we examine how these positions evolve with the cluster's dynamical age. Our results highlight how the initially more centrally concentrated P2 drives a steeper radial gradient and a steeper MF slope in the outer regions, which subsequently affects the global cluster values. Motivated by recent observational studies suggesting that a bottom-light IMF may better describe massive globular clusters, as well as measurements of a flatter P2 MF slope in the outer regions of 47 Tucanae, we additionally explore a three-parameter bottom-light IMF and a simulation in which the P1 IMF differs from the P2 IMF, with the P2 IMF flatter than that of the P1 in the lower mass range.

</details>


### [21] [oMEGACat. XI. Kinematic Characterization of Omega Centauri's Subpopulations: A Flattened Primordial Component](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15866)
*Peter J. Smith,N. Neumayer,A. C. Seth,C. Clontz,M. Häberle,S. O. Souza,Z. Wang,G. van de Ven,M. S. Nitschai,M. Libralato,A. Feldmeier-Krause,S. Kamann,F. I. Aros,R. Leaman,A. Bellini,R. Pechetti,M. Alfaro-Cuello*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 结合HST自行与MUSE视向速度，发现ω Cen染色体图上划分的两类星族运动学结构截然不同：原初星族呈显著扁平的弥散场（ε≈0.7），富集星族则近乎各向同性，暗示原初星保留了前身核的扁平轨道结构，而富集星以密集团块方式向内落或中心爆发形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ω Cen很可能是被银河系吸积的矮星系剥离后的核心，且是少数可分解为单星的星系核之一。其弛豫时间长，恒星运动学可能仍保留核形成过程的记忆。作者希望利用多星族（染色体图、金属丰度、年龄）与运动学的对照，检验不同星族是否具有不同的动力学结构，从而约束其组装历史。

Method: 将oMEGACat巡天的HST自行与MUSE视向速度联合，在half-light半径内按染色体图、金属丰度和年龄划分星族样本；分别测量各子样本的视向速度与自行弥散场、运动学椭率ε=1−σ_minor/σ_major、转动信号，并进行交叉验证（两类数据独立复核）。

Result: 染色体图划分出两个动力学上可区分的成分：原初星族弥散场扁平常，ε≈0.7（σ_minor/σ_major≈0.8），在视向速度与自行中均独立重现；富集星族在视向速度中与ε=0一致，在自行中也不比全样本更扁平；两者转动差异不明显。按金属丰度分箱未见可比结构，因为每箱混合了两类成分；年龄—金属丰度样本仅覆盖核心区，无法检验扁平性。最富集（P2）星族现今的中心集中度更高。

Conclusion: 结果支持如下图景：原初星族保留了前身星系核的扁平轨道结构，而富集星族或以密集团块形式向内落、或在紧凑且中心集中的星爆中形成；P2星族现今的中心集中说明其形成时即比原初星族更靠近中心，这与具体组装情景无关。

Abstract: Omega Centauri ($ω$ Cen; NGC 5139), the likely stripped nucleus of a dwarf galaxy accreted by the Milky Way, is one of the few galactic nuclei resolvable into individual stars. Because of its long relaxation time, the kinematics of its stars may still retain memory of how this nucleus formed. We combine HST proper motions and MUSE line-of-sight velocities from the oMEGACat survey to compare populations defined by abundance variations (the chromosome map), metallicity, and age within the half-light radius. The chromosome-map division separates two dynamically distinct components. The primordial stars have a flattened dispersion field, with kinematic ellipticity $ε\approx 0.7$ ($σ_{\rm minor} / σ_{\rm major} \approx 0.8$), which we recover independently in the line-of-sight velocities and the proper motions. The enriched stars are consistent with $ε=0$ in the line-of-sight velocities and are no more flattened than the full sample in the proper motions, and the two components show no strong difference in rotation. Metallicity bins show no comparable structure, because each bin mixes stars from both components, and the age-metallicity sample covers only the cluster core, where it cannot test for the flattening. The present-day central concentration of the most enriched (P2) stars suggests that they formed more centrally concentrated than the primordial stars, independent of assembly scenario. These results favor a picture in which the primordial stars retain the flattened orbital structure of the progenitor, while the enriched stars fell inward as dense clumps or formed in compact, centrally concentrated starbursts.

</details>

TL;DR: 该论文基于Euclid DR1强引力透镜样本，利用PyAutoLens去混叠测光、Phosphoros测光红移与Prospector SED拟合，构建了9538个星系尺度强透镜星系的光度红移与物理性质均匀星表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid大幅扩展强透镜样本（较以往巡天增加两个数量级），但常规星表测光会把前景透镜星系与背景源的光混合，导致星系参数不可靠，因此需要去混叠的均匀测光与物理性质表征。

Method: 对Euclid DR1强透镜样本中的9538个星系尺度透镜，使用PyAutoLens对Euclid与地基图像做透镜建模以获得去混叠多波段测光；用Phosphoros模板拟合估计测光红移；用Prospector做SED拟合，采用灵活的非参数恒星形成历史与Salpeter IMF，推断恒星质量、SFR、恒星年龄与金属丰度；并通过与光谱红移及外部星系巡天对比评估参数稳健性。

Result: 样本红移范围0.13≲z_phot≲1.98，中位数z_phot=0.68(+0.33/−0.29)；以大质量宁静星系为主，M*范围10^10–10^12.1 M☉，中位数log10(M*/M☉)=11.39(+0.29/−0.33)。

Conclusion: 该星表是Euclid时代首批大规模强透镜星系表征之一；去混叠测光结合均匀的测光红移与SED拟合，为星系演化、透镜星系标度关系及恒星质量与总质量关联研究奠定基础。

Abstract: The Euclid mission is revolutionising the study of strong gravitational lenses by providing high-resolution imaging over an unprecedented survey area, increasing the number of known lenses by two orders of magnitude compared to previous surveys. Characterising this sample of lens-galaxies requires accurate measurements of their physical properties. However, standard catalogue photometry often blends the light from the foreground lens and the lensed background source, limiting the reliability of derived galaxy parameters. We present a homogeneous catalogue of photometric redshifts and physical properties for 9538 galaxy-scale strong lenses identified in Euclid's DR1 strong-lens sample. We use deblended multiwavelength photometry obtained from the lens modelling of Euclid and ground-based images with PyAutoLens. The photometric redshifts of the lens galaxies are estimated using the template-fitting code Phosphoros. The stellar masses, SFRs, stellar ages, and metallicities are inferred through SED fitting with Prospector, adopting a flexible non-parametric SFH and a Salpeter IMF. The robustness of the inferred parameters is assessed through comparisons with spectroscopic redshifts and external galaxy surveys. The sample of lenses spans a broad redshift range of $0.13\lesssim z_{\rm phot} \lesssim 1.98$, with a median $z_{\rm phot} = 0.68^{+0.33}_{-0.29}$. The sample is dominated by massive quiescent galaxies with a $M_\ast$ range $[10^{10},\,10^{12.1}]\,M_\odot$ and a median $\log_{10}(M_\ast/M_\odot) = 11.39^{+0.29}_{-0.33}$. This catalogue represents one of the first large-scale characterisations of strong-lens galaxies in the Euclid era. By combining deblended photometry with homogeneous photometric redshift and SED-fitting analyses, it provides a foundation for studies of galaxy evolution, lens-galaxy scaling relations, and the connection between stellar and total mass in strong lenses.

</details>

*Clara M. Pennock,Jacco Th. van Loon,Mohammed Iddrisu Nlowie,Elizabeth Joy Leese,Maja Nowaczyk,Heinz Andernach,Duncan Farrah,Valentin D. Ivanov,Michał J. Michałowski,Joana M. Oliveira,Jahang Prathap,Syed F. Rahman,Mara Salvato,Tayyaba Zafar*

TL;DR: 该文利用无监督机器学习（t-SNE）结合光学到射电的多波段测光，在大小麦哲伦云中分类了8308个源，成功区分恒星与河外源、AGN与星系，并用SALT新光谱发现红移高达3.3的AGN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麦哲伦云拥有丰富的全电磁波段数据，但其高密度星场使河外源提取十分困难，需要有效方法从密集的恒星源中分离出河外天体（AGN、星系、耀变体等）。

Method: 采用无监督机器学习算法t-SNE，对VMC（VISTA巡天）与EMU（ASKAP射电巡天）联合探测到的大麦哲伦云6652个源和小麦哲伦云1356个源进行分类；以光学至射电的多波段测光与颜色作为特征，在二维t-SNE图上聚类相似源；再用已有分类的源将簇划分为五类：AGN、低红移星系、耀变体、尘埃恒星天体（如年轻恒星体、行星状星云等）和高自行恒星。

Result: 文献中已有的测光分类与所识别的特定簇相吻合，并揭示了t-SNE图上此前未分类的区域；结合SALT新光谱，发现红移高达3.3的新AGN以及麦哲伦发射线天体。

Conclusion: 无监督机器学习与光学至射电多波段测光的结合能有效区分恒星与河外源、AGN与星系，是处理拥挤星场河外源分类的有力工具，并可拓展至新AGN与发射线天体的发现。

Abstract: The Magellanic Clouds benefit from a wealth of data across the electromagnetic spectrum, but extracting extragalactic sources from these crowded fields is a less than straightforward endeavour. We used an unsupervised machine learning algorithm (t-distributed stochastic neighbourhood embedding; t-SNE) to classify 6,652 and 1,356 sources in the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds, respectively, that were detected in the VISTA Survey of the Magellanic Clouds (VMC) and the Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU) survey with the Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP). Multi-wavelength photometry and colours from optical to radio were used as features to cluster similar sources together in 2D space on a t-SNE map. We used previously classified sources to separate the clusters into five classes: AGN, low-redshift galaxies, blazars, dusty stellar objects (e.g. young stellar objects, planetary nebulæ etc) and high proper-motion stars. Previous photometric classifications from the literature match with specific clusters identified, as well as provide insight on areas of the t-SNE map for which there are no previous classifications. This work shows that the combination of unsupervised machine learning with photometry from optical to radio has proven adept at separating the stellar from the extragalactic, and the AGN from the galaxies. Our associated new spectroscopy with SALT includes new discoveries of AGN up to redshift 3.3 and Magellanic emission-line objects.

</details>

Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: Euclid DR1中给出15 072个高置信度强引力透镜候选体（6073个A级、8999个B级），另有9010个C级候选，构成迄今最大的均匀高分辨率星系尺度强透镜样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前强引力透镜样本规模小、来源异质、多为地面低分辨率观测，被视为"稀有发现"而难以支撑统计性天体物理与宇宙学研究；Euclid的高分辨率宽场成像为将其转变为稳健统计工具提供了契机。

Method: 在DR1约9亿源中，对通过目录筛选的6800万源运行定制发现流程：融合5个机器学习模型的评分，并结合约7000名公民科学家与67位强透镜专家的目视分类；同时为A+B级系统提供自动透镜模型，并测量偏转星系的光度红移与星族性质。

Result: 除经典爱因斯坦环外，还包含84个双源面透镜候选和1000余个盘状透镜候选，以及数百个透镜化AGN/类星体候选。偏转星系多为大质量宁静星系，log10(M*/M⊙)=11.39(+0.29/-0.33)（Salpeter IMF）；光度红移z=0.68(+0.33/-0.29)，高于以往地面样本；典型爱因斯坦半径0.85(+0.44/-0.24)角秒。

Conclusion: 该目录以超过以往所有目录之和的高置信度候选体，确立了均匀、科学就绪的强透镜数据集；按面积外推，Euclid宽场巡天预期将获得逾10万个高置信度强透镜候选。

Abstract: We present 15 072 high-confidence strong lens candidates in Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1), spanning 2000 deg$^2$. These comprise 6073 grade A ('sure lens') and 8999 grade B ('probable lens') systems, which we publish alongside a further 9010 grade C ('possible lens') candidates. With more high-confidence candidates than all previous catalogues combined, this sample establishes a large, homogeneous data set of galaxy-scale strong lenses with high-resolution imaging, transforming strong lensing from a field of rare discoveries to a robust statistical tool for astrophysics and cosmology. Of the roughly 900 million sources in DR1, we searched the 68 million passing our catalogue selection using a bespoke discovery pipeline that synthesises scores from five machine-learning models with visual classifications from around 7000 citizen scientists and 67 strong lensing experts. Beyond classical Einstein rings, our catalogue includes 84 candidate double-source-plane lenses and over 1000 candidate disc lenses, two exotic classes with few previously known examples, as well as several hundred candidate lensed AGN and quasars. We deliver automated lens models for the combined grade A+B systems, and photometric redshifts and stellar population properties for the deflector galaxies, providing immediate science-ready data products. We find that the majority of the deflector galaxies are massive and quiescent, with stellar masses of $\log_{10}(M_\ast /M_\odot) = 11.39^{+0.29}_{-0.33}$ (Salpeter initial mass function; 16th-84th percentile range). The deflector galaxies span a range of photometric redshifts ($z=0.68 ^{+0.33}_{-0.29}$), extending to higher redshifts than previous ground-based lens samples, and the lenses have a typical Einstein radius of $0.85 ^{+0.44}_{-0.24}$ arcsec. Extrapolating by area, we expect over 100 000 high-confidence strong lens candidates in the Euclid Wide Survey.

</details>


### [25] [A statistical study of ionized outflows in low-mass galaxies from DESI DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15884)
*Michael Romano,Amélie Saintonge,Chiara Circosta,Claudia Cicone,Darko Donevski,Dirk Scholte,Bendix Hagedorn*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 基于DESI DR1，对z<0.45、低质量(~5800个矮星系)星系的电离气体外流做统计分析，测得质量负载因子中位数η~0.01，电离相仅占总外流质量的一小部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量星系中反馈驱动的外流对其重子循环与演化至关重要，但缺乏大样本、统计意义上的电离外流测量；同时宇宙学模拟预测的负载因子与观测存在量级差异，需要观测检验。

Method: 利用DESI DR1光谱，以[OIII]与Hα发射线证认z<0.45、log(M*/M⊙)≲10的低质量星系中~5800个具有电离外流特征的样本，按BPT分类为恒星形成、复合与AGN宿主；计算质量负载因子η、外流速度及各相/堆叠光谱对比。

Result: η范围为0.001–1，中位数~0.01，与同质量段既有研究一致，但比宇宙学模拟低1–2 dex；≳80%情况下外流速度足以增丰CGM，逃逸至IGM主要在复合/AGN宿主中高效；η随恒星质量下降，趋势与模拟一致但归一化偏低；堆叠谱的η系统性更低，但与个体探测的差异随偏离恒星形成主序程度增大而减小；η与sSFR正相关。

Conclusion: 电离外流在低质量星系重子循环中扮演重要角色，但仅追踪总外流质量的一小部分；增强的恒星形成与爆发性（burstiness）调控反馈效率并塑造星系风；需多相外流研究才能完整理解星系演化中的反馈。

Abstract: We present a statistical analysis of ionized gas outflows in a large sample of low-mass ($\mathrm{log(M_{*}/M_{\odot})\lesssim10}$) galaxies at $z<0.45$ from the first data release of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI DR1). Using [OIII] and H$α$ emission lines, we identify $\sim5800$ dwarf galaxies with robust signatures of ionized outflows. Most are star-forming systems above the star-forming main sequence, with a smaller fraction of composites and AGN hosts. The derived mass-loading factors span $η=0.001-1$, with a median $η\sim0.01$, consistent with previous studies at similar stellar masses. These values are $1$--$2$ dex lower than predicted by cosmological simulations, suggesting that the ionized phase traces only a minor fraction of the total outflow mass. Outflow velocities are sufficient to enrich the circumgalactic medium in $\gtrsim80\%$ of cases, while escape into the intergalactic medium appears efficient mainly in composite and AGN hosts. We find that mass-loading factors decrease with stellar mass, broadly reproducing simulated trends despite the normalization offset. Comparing individual detections with stacked spectra of the general low-mass star-forming population, we find systematically lower mass-loading factors in the stacks, although the difference decreases toward larger offsets above the star-forming main sequence. Together with a positive correlation between mass-loading factor and specific star-formation rate, this suggests that enhanced star formation and burstiness regulate feedback efficiency and shape galactic winds. Our results highlight the role of ionized outflows in the baryon cycle of low-mass galaxies and the need for multiphase studies to fully understand feedback in galaxy evolution.

</details>

Verza,P. Vielzeuf,E. Vilenius,S. Vinciguerra,M. von Wietersheim-Kramsta,M. Walmsley,N. A. Walton,L. Wang,A. H. Wright*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在Euclid DR1数据中识别约15000个高质量星系—星系强透镜候选体的多阶段搜索流程，并测得对A级系统完备度约0.73、对A+B级约0.34。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强透镜是内禀稀有的系统，而DR1数据量极其庞大，单一方法难以兼顾召回率与人工检查成本，因此需要设计能最大化候选体数量、同时最小化公民科学家与研究者检查负担的搜索策略。

Method: 采用分阶段搜索：(1) 星表层面的筛选；(2) 机器学习与集成（ensemble）搜索；(3) 两阶段公民科学搜索；(4) 由强透镜研究者对候选体分级。整个流程依赖神经网络对超过6800万个天体进行自动分类，公民科学家对30万个天体完成逾450万次分类，研究者人工检查超过3万个天体；并对研究者分级引入校准以校正个体审查者偏差，同时借助ECLIPSE-C的透镜建模辅助判定候选体性质。

Result: 在综合搜索中，对A级（“确定”）系统的完备度为0.73_{-0.15}^{+0.08}，对A+B级（“确定”+“可能”）系统的完备度为0.34_{-0.06}^{+0.05}，即识别出DR1区域内大多数确定的强透镜系统，其余多为更暗弱或更模糊的案例。

Conclusion: 多阶段方法能在最大化识别透镜数量的同时降低公民科学家与研究者的检查需求；研究者分级仍具主观性，需通过校准校正个人偏差，并结合透镜建模提高候选体判定可靠性。本文属于ECLIPSE系列，聚焦DR1候选体发现流程，发现透镜的总体概览见ECLIPSE-A。

Abstract: We present the method used to identify the $15\,000$ high-grade galaxy-galaxy strong lens candidates in Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1). Given that lenses are intrinsically rare systems and the DR1 data set is vast, this lens search was conducted in a number of stages: a catalogue-level cut; a machine-learning and ensemble search; a two-stage citizen-science search, and finally grading of lens candidates by strong lens researchers. In this paper, we describe each search stage and their respective results. This search was enabled by the automated classification of over 68 million objects by neural networks, more than 4.5 million classifications of $300\,000$ objects by citizen-scientist volunteers, and the inspection of over $30\,000$ objects by strong lens researchers. We find this multi-stage approach helps maximise the number of lenses identified, while minimising inspection requirements of both citizen scientists and strong lens researchers. We measure a completeness in our comprehensive search of $0.73_{-0.15}^{+0.08}$ to grade A ('definite') systems and $0.34_{-0.06}^{+0.05}$ to grade A+B ('definite' + 'probable') systems. Our search therefore identifies the majority of the definitive strong lens systems in the DR1 area with the remainder likely to be fainter or more ambiguous cases. We find that researcher grading remains subjective; in this work we implement calibration to account for biases of individual inspectors and highlight the benefit of lens modelling (ECLIPSE-C) in determining the nature of a lens candidate. This paper forms part of the ECLIPSE series, here focussing on the discovery pipeline used to identify lens candidates in Euclid DR1; an overview of the lenses discovered is presented in ECLIPSE-A.

</details>

Walton,L. Wang,J. Wilde,A. H. Wright,D. Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 对Euclid DR1的15072个强透镜候选体进行自动化建模，成功率为A级67.5%、B级43.1%，并公开发布ECLIPSE星表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系尺度强透镜可精确测量星系质量分布、提供遥远星系放大图像并作为宇宙学探针，但以往研究仅局限于数百个透镜，自动化建模难以规模化，亟需在Euclid级样本上验证可扩展的自动化建模流程。

Method: 使用PyAutoLens-JAX对Euclid DR1的15072个强透镜候选体做自动化建模；采用等温椭球质量模型加外部剪切，分别以MGE、像素化和Sérsic三种源表示进行三次拟合，实现前景透镜光减除、自适应Delaunay源重建与去混合测光；由20余名专家目视评估，模型按至少75%评审者一致的分类定级。

Result: A级（确信）6073个中，4098个（67.5%）建模成功，580个（9.6%）失败，317个（5.2%）为多星系透镜，15个（0.2%）为双源面透镜，7个（0.1%）存疑，1032个（17.0%）无共识，24个（0.4%）模型不完整；B级（可能）8999个成功率降至43.1%，主要因无共识比例升至37.6%，而一致失败率为12.9%；成功建模系统有效爱因斯坦半径中位数0.85角秒，I_E波段源总放大率中位数8.1。

Conclusion: 研究表明自动化建模可扩展到Euclid样本规模，通过ECLIPSE星表公开发布成功模型、推断属性及放大源重建，为群体级强透镜科学奠定基础。

Abstract: Galaxy-scale strong lenses provide precise measurements of galaxy mass distributions, magnified views of distant galaxies, and a competitive cosmological probe. Historically, such studies have been limited to hundreds of lenses, with automated modelling methods proving difficult to scale. We present automated lens modelling of 15 072 strong-lens candidates from Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1), over an order of magnitude larger than any previously modelled sample. Using PyAutoLens-JAX, we perform three fits with multi-Gaussian expansion (MGE), pixelised, and Sérsic representations of the source, adopting an isothermal ellipsoid mass model with external shear. These fits provide foreground lens-light subtraction, adaptive Delaunay source reconstructions, and deblended photometry. More than 20 expert inspectors visually assess the resulting models, and each model is assigned the classification on which at least 75% of its inspectors agree. Of the 6073 grade A (secure) lens candidates, inspectors classify 4098 (67.5%) as successfully modelled, 580 (9.6%) as failures, 317 (5.2%) as multi-galaxy lenses, 15 (0.2%) as double-source-plane lenses, and 7 (0.1%) as unsure if lens, while 1032 (17.0%) reach no consensus and 24 (0.4%) have incomplete models. For the 8999 grade B (probable) candidates, the modelling success rate falls to 43.1%, primarily reflecting an increase in cases without consensus (37.6%), rather than a comparable increase in agreed failures (12.9%). Successfully modelled grade A and B systems have a median effective Einstein radius of 0.85 arcsec and a median total $I_E$-band source magnification of 8.1. We publicly release the successful lens models, their inferred properties, and magnified-source reconstructions through the ECLIPSE catalogue, demonstrating automated modelling at Euclid sample sizes and enabling population-level strong-lensing science.

</details>


### [28] [Where Do Population~III Stars Form? Unveiling Late PopIII-Forming Environments with CampFIRE-6k](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15915)
*Elizabeth Bryce,Alessandra Venditti,Jenna Samuel,Volker Bromm,Julian B. Muñoz*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用CampFIRE-6k宇宙学zoom-in模拟研究PopIII恒星形成历史，发现其形成率在z~6之前近似恒定（约10^-3.5 M☉ yr^-1 cMpc^-3），且晚期可出现在原子冷却晕中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PopIII恒星被认为主要在宇宙黎明时期的小质量暗物质晕中形成，之后被PopII取代；但模拟显示由于宇宙化学增丰的不均匀性，PopIII可能在再电离时期（z~6-10）以低速率继续形成。然而其形成速率与典型环境仍存在很大不确定性。

Method: 采用CampFIRE-6k宇宙学zoom-in模拟，追踪至z~6，系统考察PopIII恒星的形成历史与所处环境（包括晕质量与冷却模式）。

Result: PopIII恒星在z=6之前以近乎恒定的速率形成，约为10^-3.5 M☉ yr^-1 cMpc^-3；晚期还在原子冷却晕（M_halo~10^7-10^8 M☉）中形成。

Conclusion: 晚期PopIII恒星形成持续至z~6，形成率近似不随红移变化，且不完全局限于微型晕，也可在更大质量的原子冷却晕中发生，表明化学增丰的不均匀性使PopIII形成延续到再电离时期。

Abstract: Population III (PopIII) stars are the first generation of metal-free stars, thought to form in minihalos ($M_\mathrm{halo} \sim10^5-10^6\,\mathrm{M_{\odot}}$) at Cosmic Dawn ($z\gtrsim15$) before largely giving way to Population II stars formed from the newly enriched medium. Simulations show, however, that PopIII stars may continue forming at low rates through the Epoch of Reionization (EoR, $z \sim 6 - 10$), due to the inhomogeneous enrichment of the Universe. Nonetheless, significant uncertainty remains on the rates and typical environments of late PopIII formation. We use the CampFIRE-6k cosmological zoom-in simulation to examine PopIII star formation history and environments through $z\sim6$. We find that PopIII stars form at a near constant rate ($\sim 10^{-3.5} ~\mathrm{M_{\odot}\,yr^{-1}\,cMpc ^{-3}}$) through $z=6$, including within atomic-cooling halos ($M_\mathrm{halo}\sim 10^7-10^8\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$) at late times.

</details>


### [29] [Anatomy of LyC Escape in MEGATRON: Connecting Feedback Physics to Demographics, Leakage Timescales, and Explorations of ISM Density as a Tracer of Escape Fraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15931)
*Nicholas Choustikov,Harley Katz,Taysun Kimm,Matthew J. Hayes,Aaron Smith,Crystal L. Martin,Shyam H. Menon,Larrance Xing,Joki Rosdahl,John Chisholm,Aayush Saxena,Martin P. Rey,Adrianne Slyz,Julien Devriendt,Alex J. Cameron,Anatole Storck,Corentin Cadiou*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用MEGATRON zoom模拟套件研究高红移星系LyC逃逸率：反馈物理同时调控UV源计数、f_esc与逃逸电离光度，二者差异由星暴与LyC逃逸之间的延迟决定；[O II]双线电子密度可作为f_esc诊断，而UV斜率定标会高估年轻星系的N_ion,esc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束高红移星系的Lyman连续谱逃逸分数是将JWST揭示的丰富星系种群与宇宙再电离联系起来的必要环节，但反馈物理如何同时影响UV源计数、逃逸率与再电离历史尚不清晰。

Method: 采用MEGATRON zoom模拟套件，包含细致的辐射/化学处理与多种反馈方案，系统比较不同反馈物理下的UV源计数、LyC逃逸分数与逃逸电离光度；分析ISM结构随f_esc的演化序列，并评估[O II]λ3727双线电子密度与UV斜率两类f_esc诊断。

Result: 关键量由"星暴—逃逸延迟"主导：早期高效反馈与低密度环境使光子在超新星爆发前、恒星仍强电离时逃逸。UV亮星系较少的模型逃逸更早、再电离更早；UV源更丰富的模型则LyC发射延迟。UV暗星系随f_esc增大呈共同ISM演化序列：1%≤f_esc≤50%的泄漏者显示强光致电离与SNe反馈证据及星暴后恒星形成历史，f_esc≥50%的极端泄漏者已清除冷/致密ISM。[O II]双线电子密度是f_esc的候选诊断，H II区周围低密度对应更强泄漏，且能追踪f_esc的早期演化；UV斜率定标对单个年轻星系可高估N_ion,esc达两个数量级。

Conclusion: 解释UV源计数在再电离背景下的含义时，必须考虑LyC泄漏的延迟占空比；[O II]电子密度优于UV斜率，可作为f_esc及其早期演化的诊断工具。

Abstract: Constraining the Lyman Continuum (LyC) escape fraction ($f_{\rm esc}$) of high-redshift galaxies is essential to connect the abundant populations revealed by \textit{JWST} to cosmic reionization. We investigate this interplay using {\small MEGATRON}, a suite of zoom simulations with detailed radiation/chemistry and varied feedback prescriptions. We find that feedback physics simultaneously regulates UV source counts, LyC escape fractions, and escaping ionizing luminosities ($\dot{N}_{\rm ion,esc}$), producing different reionization histories. These differences are governed by the delay between a starburst and LyC escape: efficient early feedback and lower-density environments enable photons to escape while pre-supernovae stellar populations remain strongly ionizing. Consequently, models that produce fewer UV-bright galaxies yield earlier LyC escape and reionize sooner than models with more abundant UV sources and delayed LyC emission. Furthermore, UV-faint galaxies follow a common physical sequence in ISM structure with increasing $f_{\rm esc}$ irrespective of feedback physics: stronger leakers ($1\%\leq f_{\rm esc}\leq 50\%$) exhibit evidence of strong photoionization and SNe feedback alongside post-starburst star formation histories, while extreme leakers ($f_{\rm esc}\geq 50\%$) have also cleared their cold/dense ISM. We find that electron densities inferred from the $\rm [O\,{II}]\,λ\lambda3727$ doublet are a candidate diagnostic of $f_{\rm esc}$, with lower densities around $\rm H~{\small II}$ regions selecting stronger leakers. Moreover, this density can trace the early evolution of $f_{\rm esc}$, whereas UV slope-based calibrations over-predict $\dot{N}_{\rm ion,esc}$ for individual young galaxies by up to two orders of magnitude. Overall, the delayed duty cycle of LyC leakage must be accounted for when UV source counts are interpreted in the context of reionization.

</details>


### [30] [Mass Loss Rates of Evolved Stars in Isolated Dwarf Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15948)
*Narges Rostami,Hamidreza Mahani*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用SEDust流程拟合SED，得到NGC 6822中AGB星总质量损失率(3.7±1.1)×10⁻³ M☉/yr，对应尘埃注入率约2.0×10⁻⁵ M☉/yr，不足以解释该星系现有尘埃储量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验渐近巨星分支（AGB）星能否作为孤立矮不规则星系NGC 6822现今尘埃储量的唯一来源。

Method: 采用自动化流程SEDust，对AGB星的光谱能量分布（SED）进行拟合，估算其恒星质量损失率与尘埃产生率。

Result: AGB星群总质量损失率为(3.7±1.1)×10⁻³ M☉/yr；取气尘比200时尘埃注入率为2.0×10⁻⁵ M☉/yr。若持续10 Gyr，累计尘埃约2×10⁵ M☉，低于Herschel观测估计的2.7×10⁵ M☉。

Conclusion: 仅靠AGB星无法解释NGC 6822现今的尘埃储量，需要额外尘埃来源或过程，例如星际介质中的尘埃生长。

Abstract: We derive the mass-loss rates of asymptotic giant branch (AGB) stars in the isolated dwarf irregular galaxy NGC 6822 using SEDust, an automated pipeline that fits spectral energy distributions (SEDs) to estimate stellar mass-loss and dust-production rates. The total mass-loss rate of the AGB population is $(3.7 \pm 1.1) \times 10^{-3} M_\odot\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. For a gas-to-dust ratio of 200, this corresponds to a dust injection rate of $2.0 \times 10^{-5} M_\odot\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. If sustained over 10 Gyr, this rate would yield approximately $2 \times 10^{5} M_\odot$ of dust, which is lower than the dust mass of $2.7 \times 10^{5} M_\odot$ estimated from Herschel observations. AGB stars alone therefore cannot account for the present-day dust reservoir of NGC 6822, and additional sources or processes, such as dust growth in the interstellar medium, are required.

</details>

TL;DR: 利用Euclid Wide Survey首批数据（约1900 deg²）和等时线滤波技术，在10 kpc至数Mpc范围内探测到15个新分辨的恒星系统：5个新发现（3个在银河系晕、2个在本星系群外），10个此前仅作为未分辨源编目的矮星系；并测得距离、半径、椭率、位置角、绝对星等和恒星质量等参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低表面亮度、超暗弱恒星系统（超暗矮星系、球状星团、矮星系伴星）在浅的地基巡天中难以分辨，缺乏可靠距离测量，且本星系群外及极低表面亮度区域几乎没有已知星系样本。Euclid的高分辨率与深度使其可在单一均匀巡天中系统搜寻这类天体。

Method: 在Euclid Wide Survey首批数据（~1900 deg²）中进行等时线滤波（isochrone filtering）搜索，识别可分辨为恒星的系统，覆盖距离10 kpc至数Mpc；对探测到的系统测量距离模数，并对其中14个测定半光半径、椭率、位置角、绝对星等与恒星质量。

Result: 探测到15个从未被分辨为恒星的系统：5个新发现（3个在银河系晕、2个在本地群之外），10个为此前浅巡天中未分辨编目的矮星系。银河系晕中Ursa Minor II（~20 kpc，Mv~+2.0，恒星质量~40 Msun）是已知最暗弱的恒星系统之一；Fornax 7位于Fornax矮椭球星系的潮汐极限附近，可能与其物理相关；Eridanus V（~165 kpc，~550 Msun）处于球状星团与超暗矮星系在尺度-光度平面上的重叠区。本星系群外的12个矮星系距离3–5.5 Mpc，其中3个半光半径内平均表面亮度达到或超过mu_V~28 mag/arcsec²；10个为M81群候选成员，其中8个三维距离小于或相当于M81零速面半径，2个靠近外围成员NGC 4236，其一是该星系的候选卫星。

Conclusion: 结果表明Euclid在低表面亮度科学上具有变革性潜力，可在单一均匀巡天中覆盖从银河系晕到本地体积的广泛环境，为极端暗弱恒星系统的统计与结构研究提供前所未有的样本。

Abstract: We searched within the first data release of the Euclid Wide Survey (~1900 deg^2) for resolved stellar systems between distances from 10 kpc and several Mpc, using an isochrone filtering technique. We detect 15 systems that have never been resolved into stars before. Five are new discoveries, three in the Milky Way halo and two beyond the Local Group. The remaining ten are dwarf galaxies that were previously catalogued as unresolved sources in shallower ground-based surveys and lack reliable distance measures. We measured distance moduli for all 15 systems, and half-light radii, ellipticities, position angles, absolute magnitudes, and stellar masses for 14 of them. In the Milky Way halo, Ursa Minor II, at ~20 kpc, has Mv ~ +2.0 and a stellar mass of ~40 Msun, and is one of the least luminous stellar systems known. Fornax 7 is located near the tidal limit of the Fornax dwarf spheroidal galaxy and may be physically associated with it. Eridanus V lies at ~165 kpc and has a stellar mass of ~550 Msun, which places it in the region of the size-luminosity plane where globular clusters and ultra-faint dwarf galaxies overlap. Beyond the Local Group, the 12 dwarf galaxies lie at distances of 3--5.5 Mpc. Three of them reach or exceed a mean surface brightness within the half-light radius of mu_V~28 mag/arcsec^2, a regime in which almost no galaxies at these distances were previously known. Ten of them are candidate members of the M81 group: eight have three-dimensional distances from M81 smaller than or comparable to the radius of its zero-velocity surface, and two lie close to the peripheral member NGC 4236, one of which is a candidate satellite of this galaxy. These results demonstrate Euclid's transformative potential for low-surface-brightness science, enabling the study of an unprecedented range of environments from the MW halo out to the Local Volume within a single, homogeneous survey.

</details>


### [32] [B-fields And Dust in InterstelLar FiLAments Using Dust POLarization (BALLAD-POL): V. Magnetic Fields and Grain Alignment Mechanisms in Serpens Main Using JCMT/POL-2 Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16385)
*Sadhana Singh,Thiem Hoang,Pham Ngoc Diep,Nguyen Bich Ngoc,Woojin Kwon*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 基于JCMT/POL-2的850 μm尘埃偏振观测，研究Serpens Main的磁场与尘埃颗粒排列机制，发现退偏振主要源于致密区颗粒排列效率下降而非磁场缠绕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清Serpens Main中偏振度随强度下降（退偏振）的物理起源：是磁场几何缠绕，还是辐射扭矩（RAT）理论下颗粒排列效率的降低。同时评估磁场相对重力与湍流的相对重要性。

Method: 利用JCMT/POL-2 850 μm偏振数据构建磁场强度图，计算磁临界质量比与Alfvénic马赫数；对非去偏偏振度P与辐射强度I用Ricean均值模型拟合得斜率α=0.68±0.01；分析P与柱密度N(H2)、尘埃温度T_d、偏振角色散函数S的关系；在RAT理论框架下由N(H2)、T_d及磁场图计算最小排列颗粒尺寸a_align与磁弛豫强度δ_mag,sp。

Result: P与S无相关性，而排列效率代理量P×S随N(H2)增大而下降；P与P×S均随a_align增大而减小，但二者均与δ_mag,sp无相关性。磁场强度弱于重力但强于湍流。

Conclusion: Serpens Main的退偏振主要由致密区尘埃颗粒排列效率下降引起，而非磁场缠绕；磁弛豫带来的排列增强不足以补偿a_align增大所致的排列减弱。

Abstract: We investigate magnetic fields ($B$-field) and grain alignment mechanisms in Serpens Main using 850 $μ$m dust polarization observations from JCMT/POL-2. We derive a map of $B$-field strength and calculate the mass-to-flux ratio and Alfvénic Mach number, suggesting the subdominance of $B$-fields over gravity but their dominance over turbulence. Fitting the Ricean mean model to the nondebiased polarization fraction ($P$) as a function of emission intensity ($I$) yields a slope $α= 0.68\pm0.01$, revealing depolarization. To understand its origins, we examine the dependence of $P$ on gas column density ($N({\rm H}_{2})$), dust temperature ($T_{\rm d}$), and polarization angle dispersion function ($S$). We find no correlation between $P$ and $S$, whereas for $P\times S$, an observational proxy for alignment efficiency decreases with increasing $N({\rm H}_{2})$. This suggests that $B$-field tangling is not the primary cause of depolarization and instead points to reduced grain alignment in denser regions. To test this interpretation within radiative torque (RAT) alignment theory, we calculate the minimum size of aligned grains ($a_{\rm align}$) and magnetic relaxation strength ($δ_{\rm mag,sp}$) using $N({\rm H}_{2})$, $T_{\rm d}$, and our estimated $B$-field map. We find that both $P$ and $P \times S$ decrease with increasing $a_{\rm align}$, supporting reduced grain alignment as the main cause of depolarization in Serpens Main. However, neither $P$ nor $P \times S$ shows a correlation with $δ_{\rm mag,sp}$, suggesting that enhanced grain alignment due to magnetic relaxation is insufficient to compensate for the reduced alignment associated with increasing $a_{\rm align}$ toward denser regions.

</details>

TL;DR: Euclid DR1 非宇宙学科学产品由 PHZ 组织单元使用 NNPZ（基于 COSMOS 参考样本的最近邻算法）生成，经内部指标与外部数据（COSMOS2020、3D-HST、DESI）及标度关系检验，性能符合预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid 任务需为数以十亿计星系提供高精度测光红移与物理参数；宇宙学核心科学之外的非宇宙学科学同样需要自洽、可靠的数据产品，本文旨在说明 DR1 中该类产品的处理流程、数据发布与验证。

Method: 采用 PHZ 内开发的 NNPZ 最近邻算法，在色-星等空间内以具已知红移和物理参数的参考样本为模板，推断目标星系属性；参考样本基于 Euclid 辅助天区 COSMOS 观测，标签来自 COSMOS2020 多波段测光，并与核心科学结果保持一致；再结合必要的调整与先验以推广至全天区。

Result: 通过内部指标、与辅助数据集（COSMOS2020、3D-HST、DESI）的比较以及标度关系（如恒星形成主序）检验，结果显示在 Euclid 宽场巡天与深场上均与预期及 DR1 前模拟一致。

Conclusion: NNPZ 流程可获得高质量且内部自洽的非宇宙学科学数据产品，验证结果支持其用于 Euclid DR1 的科学应用。

Abstract: Euclid is a European Space Agency mission mapping the extragalactic sky, aiming to provide the most-accurate 3D view of the Universe at optical and near-infrared wavelengths, by measuring the photometric redshifts of billions of galaxies. Within the Euclid Consortium, this task is executed by the PHZ Organisation Unit. PHZ takes as input the multi-wavelength photometry of Euclid and ground-based data, and returns as output the source classification, photometric redshifts for the Euclid core science (i.e., cosmology), and photometric redshifts plus physical parameters and absolute magnitudes for non-cosmological science. As part of the first Euclid Data Release (DR1), we describe the details of the DR1 pipeline for non-cosmological science, along with the released data products, and their validation. The non-cosmological science data products are obtained with NNPZ, a nearest-neighbour algorithm developed within PHZ that infers the properties of each target galaxy from its closest matches, in colour-magnitude space, within a reference sample of galaxies with known redshifts and physical parameters. We built this reference sample from Euclid observations of COSMOS, one of the Euclid Auxiliary Fields, labelling each galaxy with redshifts and physical parameters derived from the COSMOS2020 multi-wavelength photometry, consistently with the Euclid core-science results. Combined with the adjustments and priors required to generalise the results across the entire extragalactic sky, this approach yields high-quality and internally consistent data products. We tested the performance using both internal metrics and comparisons to ancillary data sets (e.g., COSMOS2020, 3D-HST, and DESI) and scaling relations (e.g., the star-forming main sequence); the results are in line with expectations and pre-DR1 simulations for both the Euclid Wide Survey and Euclid Deep Fields.

</details>


### [34] [The Black Hole Mass of the Sunflower Galaxy NGC 5055: Direct Measurement via Stellar Kinematics with Keck/OSIRIS Laser Guide Star Adaptive Optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16490)
*Tien H. T. Ho,Dieu D. Nguyen,Alister W. Graham,Mark Durré,Hai N. Ngo,Tinh Q. T. Le,Elena Gallo,Igor V. Chilingarian,Tuan N. Le,Roberto Soria,Fan Zou,Huy G. Tong,Nghi K. N. Le,Que T. Le*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Keck/OSIRIS近红外积分场光谱（LGS-AO辅助）首次对NGC 5055中心区域进行恒星动力学测量，通过四个JAM模型拟合得到黑洞质量为4.56^{+1.73}_{-1.82}×10^6 M☉，并发现该星系可能是缺乏经典核球却具有直接动力学黑洞质量测量的稀有旋涡星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量端（尤其是晚型、无核球旋涡星系）的直接动力学黑洞质量测量极为稀少，限制了黑洞标度关系在低质量区的标定。NGC 5055被视为银河系的近邻类似体，对其开展直接动力学测量可为晚型星系黑洞标度关系提供新锚点。

Method: 采用Keck/OSIRIS近红外积分场光谱仪并借助激光导星自适应光学系统观测NGC 5055；基于CO带首吸收特征用pPXF提取二维恒星运动学，角分辨率0.045角秒；将运动学与四个JAM模型（涵盖柱状与球状速度椭球取向，以及两套基于HST存档成像的独立MGE分解）进行联合建模。

Result: 测得中心恒星速度弥散由r~1角秒处的约90 km/s陡升至中心峰值约135 km/s；拟合得到黑洞质量M_BH=4.56^{+1.73}_{-1.82}×10^6 M☉；恒星轨道接近各向同性（β≈0），符合旋转型晚型星系的典型特征；表面亮度剖面未见经典核球证据；其黑洞、核星团与总恒星质量均与银河系高度相似。

Conclusion: NGC 5055是银河系在黑洞、核星团和恒星质量方面的杰出局域类似体；若确认无核球，它将加入仅含NGC 2748、NGC 4395等少数成员的无核球但有直接动力学黑洞质量的稀有旋涡星系行列；该测量为晚型星系在黑洞质量谱低质量端的标度关系提供了新的锚点。

Abstract: We present the first stellar-dynamical supermassive black hole mass measurement in the nearby spiral galaxy NGC 5055 (M63) using near-infrared integral-field spectroscopy with Keck/OSIRIS assisted by the laser guide star adaptive optics system. The observations deliver two-dimensional stellar kinematics extracted from the CO bandhead absorption features via pPXF at an angular resolution of $0.045''$. The stellar velocity dispersion rises steeply from $\sim90~{\rm km~s^{-1}}$ at $r\sim1''$ to a central peak of $\sim135~{\rm km~s^{-1}}$, kinematically tracing the gravitational influence of a central mass concentration. Modeling these kinematics with an ensemble of four JAM models, spanning cylindrical and spherical velocity-ellipsoid alignments and two independent MGE decompositions of archival HST imaging, yields a black hole mass of $M_{\rm BH}=4.56^{+1.73}_{-1.82}\times10^6~{\rm M_\odot}$. The stellar orbits are consistent with near-isotropy ($β\approx0$), typical of rotating late-type galaxies. NGC 5055 emerges as a remarkable local analogue to the Milky Way in terms of its black hole, nuclear star cluster, and total stellar mass. The surface brightness profile of NGC 5055 shows no evidence for a classical bulge; if confirmed bulgeless, NGC 5055 would join the rare class of spirals with a direct dynamical $M_{\rm BH}$ determination in the absence of a bulge, a regime currently populated by only a handful of galaxies including NGC 2748 and NGC 4395. This measurement provides a new anchor for black hole scaling relations in late-type galaxies at the low-mass end of the black hole mass spectrum.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窄带成像对发射线形态学研究很有价值，但需专门观测且波长固定。作者希望证明Euclid NISP无缝光谱模式可在无需额外观测的前提下，合成任意中心波长的“类窄带”发射线图，从而把大规模光谱巡天数据用于二维发射线形态与统计研究。

Method: 使用2023年11月Euclid性能验证观测中COSMOS场的blue grism曝光，构建0.4<z<0.9星系的发射线图；将其应用于星形成团块识别、径向恒星形成轮廓测量、投影内禀速度梯度探测、Hα–[N II]复合线解混及光谱红移评估。统计上，对885个星系通过发射线图解混Hα–[N II]重建质–金属丰度关系，并对2634个星系测量叠加Hα径向轮廓，同时与YE恒星连续谱图像（匹配至Hα PSF）比较。

Result: 由发射线图得到的质–金属丰度关系与Euclid一维光谱测量结果一致，表明两种解混方法总体吻合。Hα径向轮廓大体跟随YE恒星连续谱，但在PSF匹配后略微更为延展，与既有文献结果相符；在四个YE尺寸分箱中平均R50,Hα/R50,YE=1.15。

Conclusion: NISP无缝光谱模式可高效产出类窄带发射线图，支持从星系内部结构到群体统计的多层次科学应用，并与一维光谱结果自洽；Hα相对恒星连续谱略为延展，提示恒星形成区分布较恒星质量分布更外扩。

Abstract: We demonstrate the capability of the slitless spectroscopic mode of the Euclid Near-Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) to produce emission-line maps analogous to narrow-band images with user-defined central wavelengths. We present the first such maps of galaxies at $0.4 < z < 0.9$, constructed from blue-grism exposures obtained during the Euclid performance-verification observations of the Cosmic Evolution Survey (COSMOS) field in November 2023. We demonstrate the use of these maps for identifying star-forming clumps, measuring radial star-formation profiles, detecting projected internal velocity gradients, deblending the H$α$--[N II] complex, and assessing spectroscopic redshift measurements. As a first statistical application, we reconstruct the galaxy mass--metallicity relation for 885 galaxies observed with the blue grism at $0.4 < z < 0.9$ by deblending the H$α$--[N II] complex in the emission-line maps, and measure stacked H$α$ radial profiles for 2634 galaxies over the same redshift range. The mass--metallicity relation is consistent with that obtained from the Euclid 1D spectroscopic measurements, demonstrating overall agreement between the two deblending approaches. The recovered H$α$ radial profiles broadly follow the $Y_{\mathrm{E}}$ stellar continuum but remain modestly more extended after matching the $Y_{\mathrm{E}}$ images to the H$α$ PSF, consistent with previous results in the literature. Across the four $Y_{\mathrm{E}}$ size bins, we measure a mean $R_{50,\mathrm{H}α}/R_{50,Y_{\mathrm{E}}}=1.15$.

*Zhijian Luo,Jianzhen Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 提出ViTMultiTask框架，用多尺度MMD损失在RKHS中约束高阶联合分布对齐，从HSC五波段成像直接推断光度红移与恒星族参数，在保持点估计精度的同时缓解深度网络MSE/Huber损失导致的"方差收缩"，更好复现双峰与重尾分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSST、CSST等大型巡天中光谱样本有限，需从多波段成像直接回归红移z与log(M*/M☉)、Dn4000、Hδ_A等恒星族参数。常规逐点损失（MSE、Huber）会引发"方差收缩"（回归到均值）效应：压缩预测方差、扭曲非线性物理流形（如双峰分布）、截断重尾分布，使预测分布无法反映真实总体分布。

Method: 构建视觉Transformer多任务框架ViTMultiTask；在逐点损失之外引入多尺度最大均值差异（MMD）损失，在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中对预测与真值的联合分布进行高阶对齐。训练集为Subaru HSC五个波段（g,r,i,z,y）成像与DESI DR1亮星系巡天光谱交叉匹配的83,846个星系，测试集为独立5,000个星系，并进行消融实验比较。

Result: 点估计精度：σ_NMAD = 0.020（z）、0.169 dex（log(M*/M☉)）、0.073（Dn4000）、1.185 Å（Hδ_A）。消融显示加入MMD损失可有效缓解方差收缩，更准确复现光谱参数分布（含双峰结构与重尾），点估计精度与基线统计一致（RMSE变化≤3.5%），灾难性离群率与基线相当。

Conclusion: 基于核的流形对齐为深度成像时代星系性质的全面推断提供了统计上严谨且实用的范式：在不牺牲点估计精度与离群率的前提下，恢复预测分布的高阶统计结构。

Abstract: Inferring photometric redshifts ($z$) and key stellar population properties ($\log(M_*/\mathrm{M}_\odot)$, $D_n4000$, and $\mathrm{H}δ_A$) directly from multi-band imaging is increasingly critical for spectroscopically limited large surveys such as LSST and CSST. However, conventional point-wise loss functions (e.g., mean squared error or Huber loss) employed in deep neural networks often induce ``variance shrinkage''---a regression-to-the-mean effect that contracts predicted variances, distorts non-linear physical manifolds (such as bimodal distributions), and truncates heavy-tail populations. Here, we present ViTMultiTask, a vision transformer framework that incorporates a multi-scale maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) loss to enforce higher-order joint distribution alignment in reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS). Trained on 83,846 galaxies from Subaru HSC five-band ($g,r,i,z,y$) imaging cross-matched with DESI Data Release 1 Bright Galaxy Survey spectra, the model achieves high point-estimate precision on an independent test set of 5,000 galaxies: $σ_{\mathrm{NMAD}} = 0.020$ for $z$, $0.169\,\mathrm{dex}$ for $\log(M_*/\mathrm{M}_\odot)$, $0.073$ for $D_n4000$, and $1.185\,\text{Å}$ for $\mathrm{H}δ_A$. Ablation experiments demonstrate that incorporating the MMD loss effectively mitigates variance shrinkage and more accurately reproduces the observed spectroscopic parameter distributions (including bimodal structures and heavy tails), while maintaining point-estimate accuracy statistically consistent with the baseline (RMSE changes $\le 3.5\%$) and catastrophic outlier rates fully comparable to the baseline. This kernel-based manifold alignment offers a statistically grounded and practical paradigm for comprehensive galaxy property inference in the deep imaging era.

</details>


### [37] [Dissecting and diagnosing the LISA Galactic binary population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16611)
*Alice E. Cravioglio,Arianna I. Renzini*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了LISA对银河系双白矮星（DWD）的已分辨与未分辨子群：可分辨性主要由chirp mass决定，空间上偏向银核；混淆背景存在chirp mass双峰结构，即使积分100年仍残留混淆底噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA将独立分辨数千个银河系DWD，但大多数仍埋在未分辨的引力波混淆前景中；哪些系统被分辨取决于双星参数、总体分布与仪器灵敏度，故已分辨样本并非银河系本征群体的无偏代表，需理解这些选择效应才能正确解读未来星表。

Method: 基于模拟的银河系DWD星表，采用迭代扣除（iterative subtraction）管线，在不同观测时长下运行，对已分辨/未分辨子群进行表征；并提出一个诊断量，用于估计在给定频段内复现特定比例混淆功率所需的最小源数目。

Result: 可分辨性主要受chirp mass驱动：高chirp mass的旋进系统显著优于低质量、外旋或单色系统，并进而影响不同组分亚型被分辨的比例；空间上已分辨源追踪银核，因其本征密度最高；混淆背景呈现内部chirp mass双峰结构，且不同频段的背景并非由同一子群体均匀产生；引入最小源数诊断，并发现即使积分100年仍存在残余混淆底噪。

Conclusion: 这些选择偏差显著，在以LISA已分辨DWD星表与混淆背景约束银河系DWD群体时必须加以考虑和修正。

Abstract: LISA will individually resolve thousands of Galactic double white dwarf (DWD) binaries, while the majority stays buried in an unresolved gravitational wave (GW) confusion foreground. Which systems get resolved depends on the binary's parameters, on the global population and on instrumental sensitivity, so the resolved population is not a representative sample of the underlying Galactic population. Interpreting LISA's future catalog therefore requires understanding these selection effects. Here we characterize the resolved and unresolved subpopulations of a simulated Milky Way DWD catalog, using an iterative subtraction pipeline applied over different observation times. Resolvability is driven primarily by chirp mass: higher chirp mass, inspiraling systems are strongly favored over lower mass, outspiraling or monochromatic ones, and this reflects on the rate with which different composition subtypes are resolved. Spatially, resolved sources trace the Galactic bulge, because it is intrinsically the densest region of the Galaxy. The confusion background shows an internal bimodal chirp-mass structure, and is not generated uniformly by the same subpopulation in different frequency bands. We introduce a diagnostic for the minimum number of sources needed to reproduce a given fraction of the confusion power in a frequency band, and show that a residual confusion floor persists even after 100 years of integration. These biases are significant and need to be accounted for when using LISA's resolved DWD catalog and the confusion background to constrain the Galactic DWD population.

</details>

*Mariana P. Júlio,Marcel S. Pawlowski,Noam I. Libeskind*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该文比较银河系、仙女座星系（M31）与HESTIA约束宇宙学模拟中本星系群类似体的卫星星系对出现率与物理性质，发现银河系近距离卫星对显著过量（配对效率约53%，模拟仅17–27%），M31则接近各向同性（约28%），多数候选对并非长期束缚双星而是瞬时机率排列或共同坠落的遗迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本星系群（MW与M31）卫星星系的成对/成团结构可能携带宿主近期吸积历史与暗物质子晕空间分布的信息，但此前缺乏对观测与宇宙学模拟类似体的系统、定量对比，尤其是区分"几何巧合配对"与"物理束缚双星"这一问题未被充分检验。

Method: 使用更新后的MW与M31卫星观测数据，并选取HESTIA约束宇宙学模拟中三个本星系群类似体作为对照；通过构建各向同性卫星分布（复现各宿主的径向分布）识别最近邻紧密关联对；结合视向速度、自行与引力束缚估计判定候选对的物理性质；并对观测数据施加 M⋆>10⁴ M⊙ 的恒星质量截断以匹配模拟的分辨率极限，保证对比公平。

Result: MW的紧密卫星对显著多于各向同性预期，配对效率约53%，约为模拟类似体（17–27%）的两倍，且该过量在恒星质量截断后依然存在；M31卫星系统与各向同性基本一致，配对效率约28%；但仅有少数候选对表现出相干运动学或可能处于引力束缚态，大多数为瞬时机率排列或共同群吸积的残留。

Conclusion: MW拥有异常丰富的相空间相关卫星群，很可能反映其近期吸积历史，偏离典型ΛCDM模拟预期；M31卫星系统则更符合标准ΛCDM框架下的典型情况。因此卫星对统计可作为探测宿主星系吸积历史与相空间结构敏感性的有效探针。

Abstract: We investigate the incidence and physical nature of satellite galaxy pairs in the Local Group using updated observational data for the Milky Way (MW) and Andromeda (M31), together with analogues from the HESTIA constrained cosmological simulations. Our comparison uses three distinct simulated Local Group analogues drawn from the HESTIA suite, allowing us to assess the observed pair statistics against multiple cosmological realisations of the Local Group environment. We identify close nearest-neighbour associations by comparing the observed satellite distributions with isotropic populations that reproduce the radial distributions of each host, and investigate their physical nature using line-of-sight velocities, proper motions, and gravitational binding estimates. We find that the MW exhibits a significant excess of close satellite associations relative to isotropic expectations, with a pairing efficiency of $\sim53\%$, nearly twice that found in the simulated analogues ($\sim17$-$27\%$). This excess persists after applying a stellar mass cut ($M_\star > 10^4\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$) to match the resolution limits of the simulations. In contrast, the M31 satellite system is broadly consistent with isotropy, with a pairing efficiency of $\sim28\%$. However, only a minority of pair candidates exhibit coherent kinematics or are likely to be gravitationally bound, indicating that most represent transient alignments or remnants of common group infall rather than long-lived binary satellites. These results suggest that the MW hosts an unusually rich population of phase-space-correlated satellites, likely reflecting its recent accretion history, whereas M31 is more consistent with typical $Λ$CDM expectations.

</details>


### [39] [Active Learning for Molecular Discovery: A Retrospective Test toward TMC-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16741)
*R. I. El-Kholy,Z. M. Hayman*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 对TMC-1的预注册回顾性测试表明：基于累积探测数据重训练的逻辑回归排序可显著优于固定排序（pooled AUC 0.867 vs 0.706），但优势在Top-100内未确立；主动学习中的不确定性采样反而劣于随机；候选生成而非排序才是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习推荐列表有助于为星际分子搜寻排定优先级，但随着探测累积，对列表重新排序（reranking）是否能优于原始固定排序，此前尚未被检验。

Method: 在TMC-1开展预注册回顾性测试：使用已发表的1,510个推荐候选（合并后1,434个）及截至2026年9月的探测记录；在2022–2025四个年度起点上，用7个已公开特征训练逻辑回归排序，并在各起点的已知探测上重新拟合；比较pooled AUC、Top-100表现、日期边界与特征方案扰动、期望搜索成本，以及不确定性采样（标准主动学习规则）；另用101个验证物种评估候选生成的覆盖度。

Result: 逻辑回归重排序pooled AUC为0.867，最佳单准则基线为0.706（差值0.160，95%区间0.095–0.230）；优势在移动日期边界和两种替代特征方案下仍成立，但在Top-100候选中未确立（12 vs 7个探测）。自我重拟合的搜索者在200次观测中位数内可覆盖63个已探测物种中的一半，最佳固定排序需332次，随机搜索期望717.5次。不确定性采样劣于随机（AUC 0.210），后发现的物种处于低预测不确定性、接近已知化学的区域。验证集101个物种中37个不在列表内，其中仅13个出现在用源数据库重建的池中（含2/11个自由基，离子全部缺失）。

Conclusion: 重新排序带来整体可测的提升，但瓶颈在候选生成而非排序；主动学习的不确定性准则不适用于此场景。作者发布100个推荐目标及代码与冻结输入用于前瞻性检验。

Abstract: Machine-learning recommendation lists can help prioritize searches for new interstellar molecules, but whether reranking such a list as detections accumulate improves on it has not been tested. We present a pre-registered retrospective test toward TMC-1, using a published machine-learning list of 1,510 recommended candidates, consolidated to 1,434 candidates, and the species reported there through September 2026. At four annual origins from 2022 to 2025, a logistic-regression ranking on seven released features, refit on the detections known at each origin, separates later detections from never-detected candidates with a pooled AUC of 0.867, against 0.706 for the best single-criterion baseline (difference 0.160; 95\% interval 0.095-0.230). The advantage persists under a shifted date boundary and two alternative feature regimes, but is not established among the top 100 candidates (12 versus 7 detections). A searcher that refits on its own observations reaches half of the 63 detected species on the list in a median of 200 observations, against 332 for the best fixed ordering and 717.5 expected for random search. Uncertainty sampling, the standard active-learning rule, performs worse than random (AUC 0.210): later detections lie at low predictive uncertainty, close to known chemistry. Of 101 species in our validation set, 37 are absent from the list, and only 13 of these appear in a pool rebuilt from its source databases, including 2 of 11 radicals and neither ion. Candidate generation, not ranking, is the main constraint. We release 100 recommended targets, with code and frozen inputs, for prospective testing.

</details>


### [40] [Magellanic Outflow and Chemistry Survey (MAGOS): the Outflow-Luminosity Scaling Relations in the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16830)
*Suinan Zhang,Kei E. I. Tanaka,Yichen Zhang,Takashi Shimonishi,Yu Cheng,Kenji Furuya,Asako Sato*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 首次系统巡天麦哲伦云（LMC/SMC，Z~0.2–0.5 Z☉）中的大质量原恒星外流，发现LMC的CO外流质量-光度关系斜率与银河系相似，而SMC外流在高光度端偏弱、标度关系更平，可能源于低金属丰度下的CO光致解离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低金属丰度环境下的大质量恒星形成，是检验恒星形成普适性、约束宇宙演化中恒星形成规律的关键。此前缺乏对低金属丰度星系中大质量原恒星外流的系统观测。

Method: 作为MAGOS巡天的一部分，用ALMA在0.85 mm波段以约0.1 pc的线性分辨率观测LMC与SMC中40个大质量年轻星体（光度(1–23)×10^4 L☉），利用CO(3–2)谱线识别与主连续谱源成协的蓝移、红移外流。

Result: 在LMC的29个源和SMC的10个源中探测到外流。LMC的CO外流在质量-光度关系上斜率与银河系相近，说明在Z~0.5 Z☉时CO仍是有效示踪剂；SMC的CO外流在高光度端明显不足，且质量、动量、能量、力与光度的标度关系比LMC和银河系更平（离散度较大）。

Conclusion: SMC结果与高光度、低金属丰度（Z~0.2 Z☉）下CO丰度降低（光致解离增强）一致，但现有数据尚不能排除外流本身较弱的可能。CO在更低金属丰度下作为外流示踪剂的可信度受限。

Abstract: Understanding massive star formation in low-metallicity environments is crucial for constraining the universality of star formation across cosmic evolution. In this Letter, we present the first systematic survey of massive protostellar outflows in the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds (LMC and SMC, $Z\sim$0.2--0.5 $Z_\odot$), as part of the Magellanic Outflow and Chemistry Survey (MAGOS). MAGOS targets 40 massive young stellar objects (MYSOs) with luminosities of (1--23)$\times 10^4$ $L_\odot$ with the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) at 0.85 mm at a linear resolution of $\sim$0.1 pc. Using CO (3--2), we identify blue- and red-shifted outflows associated with the primary continuum sources toward 29 targets in the LMC and 10 targets in the SMC. Comparing the outflow mass, momentum, energy, and force versus luminosity scaling relations in MAGOS with those in the Galaxy, we find that the LMC CO outflows have a similar slope in the mass-luminosity relation, suggesting that CO remains an effective tracer of massive protostellar outflows at $Z\sim$0.5 $Z_\odot$. In contrast, despite the large scatter, the SMC CO outflows are deficient at high luminosity and show flatter scaling relations than the LMC and the Galaxy. This is consistent with reduced CO abundances due to enhanced photodissociation at high luminosity and low metallicity ($Z\sim$0.2 $Z_\odot$), although the possibility of intrinsically weak outflow launching cannot be ruled out with the current data.

With shear included, $Σ=0.01\pm0.08$ and $S_8=0.808\pm0.025$, while $\ln B=-1.3$ favors $Λ$CDM. $H_0$ and the age of the Universe agree with $Λ$CDM for every data combination, and the SH0ES discrepancy remains at $4.4σ$. These signatures and the redshift distribution of cluster counts test the coupling independently of the amplitude of the fluctuations.

We give explicit counterexamples in a domain crossing r=0 and in the separable Ovcharenko-Podolsky extension, where an outer turning point invalidates the local test. The conic family also yields a single polynomial equation containing the critical curve in Kerr and PD. These results turn the returning image into a geometric map of radial photon propagation, independent of the emission model. (This abstract has been abridged to meet the arXiv character limit.)

Abstract: We present FOORT (Flexible Object-Oriented Ray Tracing), an open C++ code for ray-tracing images of black holes and other compact objects in arbitrary spacetimes. FOORT is designed to offer flexibility and can handle horizons, naked singularities and numerical metrics, allowing it to ray-trace numerous different metrics including Kerr and other black holes, as well as horizonless objects such as fuzzballs and boson stars. New metrics, analytical or numerical, can be added with minimal effort, and geodesics are initialized at the camera without reference to conserved quantities, so no special structure of the metric is required. Besides the intensity observed from a geometrically and optically thin equatorial disk, FOORT is set up to track a number of diagnostics along each geodesic, such as the number of equatorial crossings and the radius of closest approach. Its adaptive mesh can refine the image on any of these diagnostics, efficiently resolving narrow features such as higher-order photon rings. Accompanying Python tools convert the output into images and interferometric visibility amplitudes. We describe the main features of the code, validate it against analytic results in the Schwarzschild and Kerr spacetimes, demonstrate it on several non-Kerr metrics, and characterize its scaling with the number of OpenMP threads. FOORT is lightweight, requiring only C++17 and OpenMP, and has already been used in a number of studies of black hole and compact-object imaging. Abstract: Gibbons and Schiller proposed the maximal force conjecture in general relativity, according to which the maximum force is bounded by $c^4/G$. In this work, we derive upper bounds on the force for stable, self-gravitating, spatially regular and horizonless compact objects in $d$-dimensional general relativity, braneworld gravity, pure Lovelock gravity and Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity. Our analysis uses the connection between photon-ring structure and the stability of horizonless compact objects, adopting the absence of light rings as a criterion for stability. We investigate how the resulting force bounds depend on the spacetime dimension and on modifications to gravitational dynamics arising from extra dimensions and higher curvature corrections. We study how the bounds for such systems change under alternative higher curvature theories of gravity or in higher dimensions. Throughout this work, we consider a static and spherically symmetric spacetime sourced by anisotropic matter for which the radial and tangential pressures may differ.
