Abstract: We derive a global isotropic representation of the ultrastatic Ellis wormhole and establish which features of its electromagnetic response can, and cannot, be reproduced by a finite device. An orientation-preserving coordinate map sends the signed proper-radial line to a punctured Euclidean chart with refractive index $n(ρ)=1+a^2/(4ρ^2)$. Through the Plebański correspondence, this gives the exact impedance-matched continuum medium $\varepsilon_r=μ_r=n(ρ)\,\mathbb{1}$ and its full vector-Maxwell operator. For the ideal two-ended geometry, we derive the electromagnetic radial operator, establish its spectral properties, and express its scattering amplitudes through Jost solutions and Wronskians. Nonzero transmission into the second asymptotic end implies $|R_{\rm id}|<1$ at every real frequency, whereas a regular, lossless finite one-port has unit-modulus reflection. The reverse triangle inequality therefore yields a strictly positive lower bound on the complex reflection-amplitude mismatch. This theorem applies to the stated lossless single-port class and is not extended to lossy or multiport realizations. We compute converged multipole scattering data and establish the low-frequency threshold scaling $|T_L|^2\propto(ωa/c)^{4L+2}$, with coefficients independently validated for $L=1,2,3,4$. The transmission cross section tends to $πa^2$, while the ideal profile's total scattering cross section has a logarithmic forward divergence. We also construct a smooth finite-shell target and derive an exact finite-band constitutive-error formula for a calibrated passive Lorentz model. The constitutive benchmark is kept distinct from scattering equivalence throughout.

Abstract: Highly spinning binary black holes exhibit complex inspiral dynamics and offer a powerful opportunity to probe gravity in a stronger-field and more dynamical regime than non-spinning binaries. Using NR simulations from the SXS catalog, we first recover the injected signals within general relativity using $\texttt{IMRPhenomXPHM-SpinTaylor}$, $\texttt{IMRPhenomXPNR}$, and $\texttt{SEOBNRv5PHM}$, finding model-dependent biases in the inferred masses, spins, and spin orientations. We then analyze the same signals with the $\texttt{IMRPhenomXPHM}$-based inspiral parameterized test of general relativity framework, TIGER, and find that these waveform-systematic biases can lead to apparent deviations from general relativity, which become significant for loud signals. Our results highlight that highly spinning binaries offer powerful tests of higher-order post-Newtonian deviations, but waveform inaccuracies can lead to false signs of new physics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI是LISA的主要探测目标之一，其轨道偏心率携带天体物理起源信息，探测偏心率意义重大。但偏心率对可探测性存在相反效应：一方面使偏心波形更易区别于圆轨道波形，另一方面将信噪比重新分配到多个谐波、削弱单谐波搜索的功率。此前缺乏对该权衡的定量刻画。

Result: 偏心率在最终偏心率 e_f≳0.05 时即可与零区分；而单谐波灵敏度在 e_f≃0.1 之后开始下降。无噪声情形下，纳入能恢复至少50%总SNR的谐波可避免该灵敏度退化，但会在检测阈值标定时引入额外的统计自由度。

Conclusion: 偏心率驱动的可辨识性、谐波功率损失与额外谐波的统计代价三者之间的平衡，是未来EMRI搜索方法发展中的重要考量，对两个天体物理种群的应用展示了这些竞争效应与其偏心率分布的交互作用。

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) consist of a stellar-mass compact object orbiting a massive black hole and are among the primary targets for the future space-based gravitational-wave detector LISA. Their orbital eccentricity carries valuable information about their astrophysical origins, making its detection particularly important. However, increasing eccentricity has competing effects on detectability: it makes eccentric waveforms easier to distinguish from circular ones, but also redistributes signal power across multiple harmonics, reducing the power captured by single-harmonic searches. In this work, we quantify this trade-off within a semi-coherent framework for a source with fixed total SNR of $50$. Eccentricity becomes distinguishable from zero for final eccentricities $e_f \gtrsim 0.05$, while single-harmonic sensitivity declines beyond $e_f \simeq 0.1$. In noise-free analyses, including enough harmonics to recover at least $50\%$ of the total SNR avoids this degradation, but adds statistical freedom when calibrating detection thresholds. We apply the framework to two astrophysical EMRI populations, showing how these competing effects interact with their eccentricity distributions under simplified assumptions. The balance among eccentricity-driven distinguishability, harmonic power loss, and the statistical cost of additional harmonics is an important consideration for future EMRI search developments.

</details>


### [64] [Machine learning and high accuracy: Neural preconditioners for computational gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16106)
*Thomas Osburn,Nils Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出以图神经网络作为迭代求解器的预条件子、并用基于真实残差的损失训练的混合ML方法，高精度求解引力波与弹性波中的亥姆霍兹型稀疏线性系统，最难参数区迭代次数减少2–15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在理论物理中多用于中低精度任务，因神经网络等引入的噪声底难以满足更严格容差；高精度领域（如依赖LISA等高灵敏探测器的引力波建模）因而对ML持谨慎态度。作者希望在高精度与高速度要求下引入ML，而不牺牲精度。

Method: 采用"混合"思路：将快速但有噪声的ML机制嵌入传统数值格式中以加速收敛并最终达到或超越标准方法精度。具体地，针对非对称质量双黑洞引力波与弹性波所需的、具亥姆霍兹特征且传统迭代法难以处理的稀疏线性系统，训练图神经网络作为显著更快的预条件子；损失函数基于迭代算法的真实残差，GNN直接作用于稀疏矩阵，仅需少量物理上下文。

Result: 在参数空间中最困难的区域，神经预条件子相较传统预条件子将迭代次数降低2–15倍；由于直接操作稀疏矩阵且依赖少量物理信息，模型可泛化到训练域之外，并可通过重训练适配物理学中的新问题而无需重新设计。

Conclusion: 混合ML方法为引力波建模及其他领域兼顾高精度与高速度需求提供了新而有说服力的途径。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) developments have empowered valuable calculations in many areas of theoretical physics. Certain topics involving moderate-to-low accuracy requirements have been particularly fortunate because many ML techniques, such as neural networks, introduce a noise floor that would be difficult to penetrate with stricter tolerances. For this reason, topics requiring high accuracy can be hesitant to pursue ML. One prominent example is gravitational wave modeling, which relies on traditional methods that accommodate the high sensitivities of detectors such as LISA. However, recent progress in applied mathematics has identified hybrid methods where fast-but-noisy ML mechanisms are inserted into otherwise traditional numerical schemes to accelerate convergence in a way that ultimately meets or exceeds the accuracy of standard methods. We develop an optimized hybrid ML method to tackle a class of challenging PDEs; specifically, we demonstrate ML implementations for gravitational waves from asymmetric-mass black hole binaries and elastic waves. These require solving sparse linear algebra systems with Helmholtz features that are especially difficult for traditional iterative methods. Our new approach is to train a graph neural network (GNN) as a significantly faster preconditioner using a novel loss function based on the true residual of the iterative algorithm. For the most difficult regions of parameter space, our neural preconditioner decreases iteration counts by 2-15x compared to traditional preconditioners. Because the GNN operates directly on the sparse matrix and needs only modest physical context, it generalizes beyond its training domain and can be adapted to new problems across physics by retraining rather than redesign. Hybrid ML methods such as this offer a new and compelling avenue to handle high accuracy and speed requirements in gravitational wave modeling and beyond.

</details>


### [65] [Population inference of eccentric binary black holes using asymptotic constants of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15738)
*Chiara Anselmo,Giulia Fumagalli,Davide Gerosa*

Main category: gr-qc

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用Newton轨道角动量是相对论两体问题渐近运动常数、且在零频极限下保持有限这一已知性质，将偏心群体推断从以偏心率为变量重构为以轨道角动量为变量，并在模拟引力波数据上验证该流程。

Result: 在模拟引力波数据上成功实现了基于轨道角动量的偏心群体推断，属首个仅使用渐近运动常数的偏心群体推断示例；推断完成后可将结果传播到任意偏好的频率，以便与天体物理预言比较。

Conclusion: 以渐近运动常数（轨道角动量）作参考量可为偏心群体推断提供数学上一致、无阶段混杂的框架，结果可无损回推到任意观测频率，兼顾内部一致性与与天体物理预言的可比性。

Abstract: In standard gravitational-wave population analyses of compact binaries, source parameters are defined at a reference frequency. For quantities that evolve during the inspiral, this means constructing a population of systems at different evolutionary stages, which complicates the astrophysical interpretation of the results. The past-time infinity, or zero-frequency, limit provides a mathematically well-defined reference where all sources enter the population fit on equal footing. This approach was first proposed for spin tilts, which evolve due to spin precession but converge to constant values as binaries are back-propagated. The orbital eccentricity also evolves throughout the inspiral and approaches unity in the zero-frequency limit for all binaries, making it unsuitable as a common reference point for population analyses. On the other hand, the Newtonian orbital angular momentum is an asymptotic constant of motion of the relativistic two-body problem and remains finite in this limit. In this paper, we make use of this known asymptotic behavior to reformulate eccentric population inference in terms of orbital angular momentum rather than eccentricity and demonstrate this approach on simulated gravitational-wave data. Our work constitutes the first example of eccentric population inference using only asymptotic constants of motion. Importantly, after the inference has been performed using this consistent reference, the results can be propagated to any preferred frequency for comparison with astrophysical predictions.

</details>

*Yishen Huang,Jiongyue Li,Junbin Li,Xi-Ping Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 文章证明：在合理几何与物理假设下，光滑、渐近平直、稳态真空且描述单个旋转非退化黑洞的时空，其外通讯域与次极端Kerr解的外通讯域等距，无需解析性或小性假设。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用Harrison变换构造Einstein–Maxwell方程的一族解；结合零面积比较（null area comparison）与面积–电荷不等式，分析极限变换几何中分叉球面的性质，并借此验证Ionescu–Klainerman唯一性定理所需的技术性假设。

Result: 在ADM质量归一化为M=1的极限变换几何中，分叉球面的面积半径为1，且具有常数的电、磁通量密度；这些恒等式恰好满足Ionescu–Klainerman的技术假设，从而得到与Kerr解的等距识别。

Conclusion: 真空稳态单旋转黑洞外部时空在无解析性与无小性假设下唯一确定为次极端Kerr解，说明先前的限制性假设可被去掉。

Abstract: We show that the domain of outer communications of a smooth, asymptotically flat, stationary vacuum spacetime describing a single rotating non-degenerate black hole is isometric to the domain of outer communications of a subextremal Kerr solution under reasonable geometric and physical assumptions, without analyticity or any smallness assumptions. The method is to apply Harrison transform to construct a family of solutions of Einstein--Maxwell equations. Combining null area comparison with the area-charge inequality, we show that, in the limiting transformed geometry, the bifurcate sphere has area radius one and constant electric and magnetic flux densities, with the original ADM mass normalized to $M=1$. These identities imply the technical assumptions of Ionescu--Klainerman, yielding the identification with Kerr.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验四维情况下黑洞形成过程中的真空极化与光度行为是否与二维模型一致；二维情形由于模函数无散射，可能高估差值衰减速度，因此需要在有散射的四维时空中重新计算。

Method: 在零壳层外部、视界外部的史瓦西几何区域，计算无质量最小耦合标量场球对称模对 $\langle\varphi^2\rangle_{\rm in}-\langle\varphi^2\rangle_U$ 及 $L_{\rm in}-L_U$ 的贡献，比较 in 真空与史瓦西时空 Unruh 态；对光度差的尾项晚期行为给出解析计算验证。

Result: 两类差值在晚期均趋于零，但衰减显著慢于无模函数散射的二维情形；中间时段差值由准正规模主导，晚期由随时间四次方衰减（quartic）的尾项主导；光度差的尾项晚期结果得到解析验证。

Conclusion: 四维黑洞形成过程中 in 真空与 Unruh 态的差异虽最终消失，但其衰减受准正规模与晚期尾项控制，明显慢于二维结果；这提示二维模型不能定量外推至四维，需考虑模函数散射导致的幂律尾部。

Abstract: The vacuum polarization and luminosity are computed in a four-dimensional spacetime where a spherically symmetric null shell contracts to form a black hole. This is done by computing the contribution of the spherically symmetric modes of a massless minimally coupled scalar field to the quantities $\la φ^2 \ra_{\rm in} - \la φ^2 \ra_U$ and $L_{\rm in} - L_U$. Here the subscript ``in'' denotes the {\it in} vacuum state for the null shell spacetime and the subscript ``U'' denotes the Unruh state in Schwarzschild spacetime. The computation is done outside the null shell and outside the event horizon where the spacetime geometry is the Schwarzschild geometry. It is found that, as expected, these differences approach zero at late times. However, they do so significantly more slowly than in the two-dimensional case where there is no scattering of the mode functions. In both cases, the difference is dominated by a quasinormal mode at intermediate times and a tail term at late times that decays quartically in time. For the luminosity difference, an analytic calculation verifies the late-time result for the tail term.

*Milko Estrada*

Result: 在所研究的数值范围内，几何仅有一个事件视界、无额外内视界，中心奇点为可积型；NFW密度ρ∼r^{-1}与G_I(r)∼r结合使有效物质贡献在原点附近保持有限，自然产生核化（cored）行为而无需人工修改NFW轮廓；NFW扇区使事件视界发生移动但保持单视界结构。

Conclusion: 同一跑动耦合既支配可积的UV黑洞几何，又软化NFW源的中心引力响应，为同时处理正则黑洞与暗物质环境提供了一个自洽的现象学框架。

Abstract: We investigate an alternative ultraviolet (UV) structure for Renormalization Group improved (RG) black holes within a phenomenological framework inspired by asymptotically safe gravity. In several regular RG constructions, regularity at the origin is associated with a dS core and an additional inner horizon, raising issues of spacetime predictability and possible mass inflation instabilities. Motivated by this problem, we relate the RG scale to the radially weighted effective quantum gravitational source entering the volume integrand of the quantum corrected Ricci scalar. This prescription yields a Bessel type running Newton coupling with $G_I(r)\sim r$ in the UV, rather than the usual $G(r)\sim r^3$ behavior, and supports an integrable central singularity. The inner branch is matched through the Israel Darmois conditions to an exterior RG solution with $G_{II}\to G_0$ in the infrared, where $G_0$ is the Newton constant. Within the numerical range investigated, the resulting geometry has a single event horizon and no additional inner horizon. We then incorporate an NFW dark matter distribution coupled to the same running coupling. Since $ρ_{\rm NFW}\sim r^{-1}$ while $G_I(r)\sim r$ near the origin, the effective matter contribution entering the field equations remains finite, producing a cored effective behavior without modifying the NFW profile by hand. The gravitational decoupling algorithm keeps the quantum gravitational and dark matter sectors separately conserved within the same geometry. For the configurations considered, the NFW sector shifts the event horizon while preserving the single horizon structure. Thus, the same running coupling governs both the integrable UV black hole geometry and the softening of the central gravitational response of the NFW source.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a metric reconstruction scheme that computes the metric directly from the linearized Einstein equations for noncompact sources. Working in a traceful radiation gauge, we hierarchically solve four Einstein transport equations, with the remaining six enforced by suitable homogeneous data. Unlike previous Newman-Penrose approaches, we show how the homogeneous data are fixed, thereby closing the full set of Einstein equations. We conclude by relating our scheme to existing sourced reconstruction methods, thus establishing a connection between the Newman-Penrose and Hertz-potential approaches.

</details>


### [70] [Cubic Curvature Deformations to the Kerr Black Hole Shadow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15876)

  <summary>Details</summary>

We find that cubic curvature interactions can produce discernible deviations from the Kerr shadow, modifying its apparent size and shape. In particular, the two cubic interactions exhibit distinct effects on the shadow boundary, with the magnitude of the deviations depending sensitively on the coupling strengths and black hole spin. We validate our numerical results through independent convergence tests of the geodesic integration and shadow-boundary determination, and recover the analytical Kerr shadow in the vanishing-coupling limit. Abstract: We construct a pair of null coordinates for the Kerr-Vaidya spacetime, a nonstationary rotating black-hole geometry whose mass varies with the retarded or advanced time. The metric is naturally written in Eddington-Finkelstein-type coordinates, but the corresponding time coordinate fails to be null when the spin is nonzero, so each null coordinate must acquire a correction of the Carter type that depends on the polar angle. The canonical null one-forms obtained from the eikonal equation are not closed when the mass varies, and their closure conditions are different equations. We also show that the unextended null coordinates are singular at the horizon, where the advanced coordinate diverges logarithmically and the metric determinant vanishes.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We compare covariant and canonical relational dressings with gauge-fixing reductions for the flat FLRW minisuperspace with a homogeneous massless scalar. We distinguish covariance of the dressing map from symmetries of the relational theory: fixing a clock makes its frame fields background structures, so its reorientations act as vector fields on relational field space rather than as spacetime diffeomorphisms. This loss of relational-time reparametrization covariance permits a nontrivial relational Hamiltonian, distinct from the undressed and dressed Hamiltonian constraints, which vanish on the constraint surface. We identify the conditions under which the dressed equations reproduce the original constrained dynamics. The relational generator defines a Hamiltonian flow even when relational-time translations are not symmetries of the dressed action. In each clock frame, it corresponds to the canonical complete observable and reduced Hamiltonian on the appropriate solution space, through the Legendre map and relational evaluation. The classical descriptions are compatible but retain distinct variational principles and intermediate phase spaces; their quantum equivalence remains an open question.

Abstract: We present the first systematic parameter-estimation analysis of 42 candidate gravitational-wave signals identified in GWTC-4.0 with a probability of astrophysical origin $p_\mathrm{astro} \geq 0.5$, but a false-alarm rate of at least $1\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$, which were therefore not selected for parameter estimation in the catalogue. We use a catalogue-style analysis workflow and publicly available strain data to obtain posterior samples for these candidates, of which 29 pass our validation criteria. Of the remaining 13 candidates, seven are consistent with noise or impacted by instrumental artefacts, while six yield results that cannot be reliably interpreted.

The latter also shows mild evidence of precession, although further analysis is needed to establish the robustness of this result. We quantify the information gained from these observations using the Kullback-Leibler divergence, finding that the inferred spin parameters are generally only weakly constrained.

  Our results demonstrate that useful parameter constraints can be obtained for many candidates below the catalogue's parameter-estimation significance threshold, while highlighting the challenges of analysing marginal signals and the importance of careful validation.

</details>

Abstract: We study a quantum Otto cycle whose working medium is a qubit Unruh-DeWitt detector undergoing uniform circular motion in $(3+1)$-dimensional Minkowski spacetime, interacting with a massless scalar field in a thermal state during the isochoric strokes. We compute the detector's transition rate and determine its energy gap-dependent effective temperature, showing that the effective temperature can be higher than the ambient bath temperature in the small energy gap limit and lower in the high bath temperature limit. We then investigate how these motion-induced effective temperatures affect the operating conditions and performance of the quantum Otto cycle as a heat engine or a refrigerator. We show that the engine efficiency and refrigerator performance coefficient can exceed their standard Carnot bounds based on the bath's rest-frame temperatures, while remaining bounded by generalised Carnot limits expressed in terms of the effective temperatures experienced by the detector. In the high-temperature limit, the effective temperature coincides with that of an inertial detector moving through a thermal bath, and a comparison with the small-gap limit reveals that circular motion allows the standard Carnot bounds to be exceeded at lower speeds than in the inertial case.

Abstract: Multiparameter gravitational-wave tests of general relativity (GR) are limited by strong correlations among post-Newtonian (PN) phase deformations and the computational cost of Bayesian inference. We present DINGO-TGR, a neural posterior estimation framework that jointly infers binary-source parameters and six PN phase deformations within the TIGER parametrization, using IMRPhenomXPHM waveforms with spin precession and higher-order modes. Importance sampling reweights the neural proposal samples, while principal component analysis (PCA) identifies two well-constrained combinations of PN deformations. We validate the framework using 16 zero-noise GR injections and 12 beyond-GR injections, recovering GR-consistent posteriors in the former and leading-mode shifts consistent with the injected deformations in the latter. For GW150914 and GW250114, our results agree with independent \texttt{bilby-tgr} analyses using \texttt{dynesty}. After training, inference including importance sampling takes approximately 38 minutes and 4.4 hours, respectively, compared with 177 and 238 hours for conventional sampling. GW150914 exhibits a mild offset from GR in the leading PCA mode, whereas GW250114 is consistent with GR. To assess the significance of the GW150914 offset, we analyze 1000 GW150914-like GR injections with independent Gaussian-noise realizations and fixed detector power spectral densities. The resulting empirical upper-tail $p$-value is $11.5\%$, indicating that the observed offset does not reject the adopted GR null model at the 5\% significance level. Our results demonstrate the potential of importance-sampling-corrected neural inference for efficient multiparameter GR tests and large-scale injection-based significance assessments.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We examine the gauge structure of the Teleparallel Equivalent of General Relativity (TEGR) from the perspective of Noether's second theorem. We show that diffeomorphisms and local Lorentz transformations are the gauge symmetries of TEGR, as these are the only variational symmetries that give rise to Noether identities. We then discuss the distinct roles of these two symmetries and their implications for the gauge-theoretic interpretation of gravity. Finally, we discuss consequences of these observations for the role of translations in gauge theories of gravity and the coupling of matter in TEGR. In particular, we demonstrate that the matter coupling prescription is uniquely determined and coincides with that of general relativity, including in the case of spinor fields.

</details>

Result: 恢复效果由透镜放大与波形调制的竞争决定：较小碰撞参数通常因放大更强而改善恢复，较高横向速度因调制更快而部分抵消该增益，尤其在小碰撞参数、短源—透镜距离（像干涉显著）时；响应还依赖信号本身强度——强信号分数变化小、部分构型净损失，弱信号失配分布更宽且延伸至更负值（增强更明显）。总体研究复现了对信号强度的强依赖，不同总体间差异不大，但高速度、短源—透镜距离总体倾向更大失配。

Abstract: Continuous gravitational waves (CWs) from neutron stars beyond the Galactic Center can be lensed by the supermassive black hole in Sgr A$^*$. Since the signal lasts far longer than the lensing time delay, the two geometric-optics images overlap and interfere. Transverse source motion during the observation makes their magnifications and relative time delay evolve, producing evolving amplitude and phase modulations absent from standard CW templates. Using lensed and unlensed injections in Gaussian noise, we quantify their effect on a Hough-based semicoherent search through a semicoherent mismatch. Recovery is governed by the competition between lensing amplification and waveform modulation. Smaller impact parameters generally improve recovery through stronger magnification, while higher transverse velocities partially offset this gain through faster modulation, especially at small impact parameters and short source-lens distances, where image interference is appreciable. The response also depends on the intrinsic signal strength. Loud signals exhibit small fractional changes, with net losses in some configurations, whereas weaker signals yield broader mismatch distributions extending to more negative values, indicating greater enhancement. A population study, sampling lensing parameters from astrophysically motivated velocity and source-lens distance distributions, reproduces the strong dependence on signal strength. Differences between populations are modest, but high-velocity, short source-lens distance populations tend towards larger mismatches. Lensing effects therefore cannot be accounted for by rescaling search sensitivity with the magnification alone; reinterpreting directed Galactic Center search sensitivities and upper limits under the lensing hypothesis requires injection studies with the full lensed waveform across the lensing parameter space.

</details>

Abstract: We derive a microscopic relation between the many-body Chern number $C$ of the fermionic ground state and the braiding of fluxes in $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ gauge theory coupled to matter: under a spectral gap condition, dressed flux loops obey $W_2W_1=e^{i\fracπ{2}C}W_1W_2$ on the ground-state sector. Abstract: The Poisson $σ$-model (PSM) over a closed worldsheet $Σ$ is reinterpreted as a theory of connections on a trivial principaloid bundle $P_0 = Σ\times G \to Σ$ with the symplectic structure groupoid $(G,ω)$ integrating the Poisson target $(M,Π)$, and that of sections of the shadow bundle $F_0 = Σ\times M \to Σ$ - as defined in 2503.09886 [math.DG]. The flat directions of the PSM are explained as linearisations of automorphisms of $P_0$, modelled on maps from $Σ$ into the group of lagrangean bisections of $G$, which induce Poisson automorphisms of $(M,Π)$. The coupling takes the form of the tensor product of the two gerbes and the extended PSM is defined as the holonomy of the product gerbe. An essential generalisation of the theory is then proposed - to shadows $F$ of principaloid bundles $P$ of arbitrary topology, in keeping with the interpretation of the gauging as descent to the orbistack $M//G$. The generalisation uses local product gerbes depending on local connections over trivialisations of $F$. The $G$-equivariant structure is proven to ensure a 2-stacky descent of the local gerbes to a global one over $F$. Its holonomy is taken as the definition of the generalised PSM. A cohomological discussion of obstructions against the descent is provided. Finally, a reformulation of the generalised PSM as a field theory with a topological gauge-symmetry defect is given, in which the $G$-equivariant structure is employed - alongside the transition cocycle of $P$ - as the datum of the defect.

</details>

Abstract: The paper presents a brief overview of recent advances in the mathematical theory of evolution equations for quantum many-particle systems.

It has been shown that the concept of cumulants of operator semigroups forms the basis for non-perturbative representation of solutions to hierarchies of evolution equations that describe the state and observables.
  It is proven that the constructed dynamics of correlations underlie the description of the evolution of the state, both for finitely and infinitely many particles, governed by the BBGKY hierarchy for reduced density operators or by the hierarchy of nonlinear evolution equations for reduced correlation operators. In fact, it is demonstrated that the behavior of particle correlations governs the dynamics of states in quantum systems with an infinite number of particles.

</details>

### [86] [Metriplectic Lotka--Volterra systems with noise:interactions invisible at stationarity, visible in time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15632)

TL;DR: Lotka–Volterra系统具有内平衡点时，恰在Volterra耗散条件CA+AᵀC≤0成立时可用对数典范Poisson双向量构造度量辛结构；据波动-耗散关系加噪得乘性环境噪声，其平稳律为独立Gamma乘积（与对称性无关），相互作用仅体现在时间性质（零延迟对数协方差导数=A(C/k)⁻¹）与熵产生中，并给出一般噪声下的精确Lyapunov型恒等式。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知随机Lotka–Volterra系统的Gibbs型平稳测度多限于对称相互作用或特殊噪声；同时微生物群落中观察到Gamma分布、不相关涨落的现象常被当作无相互作用的证据。作者旨在用度量辛（metriplectic）/随机GENERIC框架厘清：平稳分布究竟能识别哪些参数、相互作用在何处可见，从而纠正"无相关即无相互作用"的推断。

Method: 1）用对数典范（log-canonical）Poisson双向量构造Lotka–Volterra的度量辛结构，能量取Volterra函数V_C，熵变量对应环境，证明其存在性当且仅当CA+AᵀC≤0；2）通过随机GENERIC的波动-耗散关系引入乘性环境噪声，协方差由AC⁻¹的对称部分固定，推导平稳Gibbs测度并显式化为独立Gamma乘积；3）分析平稳律对参数的依赖（仅定出平衡点与C/k）；4）计算零延迟对数丰度协方差的导数A(C/k)⁻¹及其反对称部分（Poisson部分），闭式给出平稳熵产生；5）对一般噪声证明精确恒等式AK+KᵀAᵀ+Q=0（K=Cov(x,log x)），据此推出独立性等价于噪声度量辛相容，并给出跨物种协方差下界；6）用捕食-被捕食模型数值模拟检验下界的紧性。

Result: 度量辛结构存在性与Volterra耗散条件等价，且可显式构造；乘性环境噪声的平稳律为独立Gamma乘积，对非对称相互作用（含捕食-被捕食）同样成立；平稳律只决定平衡点与C/k，n²维开锥内的任意相互作用矩阵（尤其是任意Poisson部分）配以相容噪声都给出与解耦随机logistic方程相同的分布，故Gamma型不相关涨落不能作为无相互作用的证据；相互作用体现在动力学中：零延迟对数协方差导数为A(C/k)⁻¹，其反对称部分即Poisson部分并承载全部平稳熵产生；一般噪声下恒等式AK+KᵀAᵀ+Q=0成立，种群独立当且仅当噪声度量辛相容，跨物种协方差有由Q到相容族距离给出的显式下界；捕食-被捕食模拟显示该下界近乎锐利。

Abstract: We show that a Lotka--Volterra system with an interior equilibrium admits a metriplectic structure, with a Volterra function $V_C$ as energy and an entropy variable for the environment, if and only if Volterra's dissipativity condition $CA+A^{\top}C\le0$ holds, and we construct it explicitly with a log-canonical Poisson bivector. Adding noise through the fluctuation--dissipation relation of stochastic GENERIC yields multiplicative environmental noise, with covariance fixed by the symmetric part of $AC^{-1}$, whose stationary law is an explicit Gibbs measure: a product of independent Gamma distributions. Unlike known Gibbs measures for stochastic Lotka--Volterra systems, this holds for nonsymmetric interactions, predator--prey included. The stationary law determines only the equilibrium and $C/k$: every interaction matrix in an open cone of dimension $n^2$, in particular any Poisson part, produces with its compatible noise the same law as uncoupled stochastic logistic equations. Gamma-distributed, uncorrelated abundance fluctuations, as observed in microbial communities, are therefore no evidence of absence of interactions. The interactions are, however, visible in time: the zero-lag derivative of the log-abundance covariance equals $A(C/k)^{-1}$, and its antisymmetric part is the Poisson part, which carries all the stationary entropy production, computed in closed form. Finally, for general noise we prove an exact Lyapunov-type identity $AK+K^\top A^\top+Q=0$ for $K=\mathrm{Cov}(x,\log x)$ at any stationary solution. It implies that the populations are independent if and only if the noise is metriplectically compatible, and gives an explicit lower bound on interspecies cross-covariances in terms of the distance of $Q$ to the compatible family. Simulations of a predator--prey model show that the bound is nearly sharp.

</details>

We also revisit the classical Lie symmetry analysis of this model in detail, concentrating on hidden infinitesimal and hidden point symmetries. Additionally, we re-examine the symmetry properties of each codimension-one reduced model and determine their point symmetry pseudogroups.

Abstract: This is the first of two papers devoted to spatial Fréchet structures on AF algebras and their derivations. Here, we study the simplest example of such a structure: the Fréchet algebra of almost-local compact operators associated with a fixed orthonormal basis. We revisit the characterization of its continuous derivations in terms of infinite matrices and their operator-theoretic implementation on rapidly decreasing vectors. Reformulating this description in the language of 0-chains, borrowed from the theory of quantum many-body systems, we establish a sufficient condition for continuity in terms of spatial growth. Finally, we construct a Fréchet-continuous symmetric derivation that does not generate a one-parameter group of automorphisms of this algebra that is strongly continuous with respect to its Fréchet topology.

</details>

Abstract: We construct two-parameter Baxter $\mathbb{Q}$-operators for the transfer matrices of noncompact open $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$ BC-type spin chains with general boundary interactions, treating both one-boundary and two-boundary models. The $\mathbb{Q}$-operators are defined as traces of general monodromy matrices over infinite-dimensional auxiliary spaces carrying principal series representations of $SL(2,\mathbb{C})$.

We prove that the general transfer matrices factorize into products of these one-parameter $Q$-operators. We provide both an algebraic derivation based on local operator identities and a diagrammatic proof using Feynman diagrammatic techniques.

We derive the system of separated difference equations for the transfer matrix eigenfunctions and show that, under the assumption of kernel factorization, it reduces to a one-dimensional difference equation coinciding precisely with the Baxter equation for eigenvalues of appropriately normalized one-parameter Baxter $Q$-operator.

</details>

Result: 建立了理想与σ-代数之间的双向对应：理想由σ-代数描述；理想的包含关系与对应σ-代数的包含关系反向；独立性对应于条件期望存在，从而获得理想的概率化特征刻画。

Conclusion: resolvent代数理想的结构可完全用概率语言表述，σ-代数框架为理想的包含与独立性提供了自然解释，加深了对KMS系统—随机过程对应下代数与概率结构一致性的理解。

Abstract: We give a probabilistic characterization of the ideals of resolvent algebras. Through the correspondence of Klein and Landau between KMS systems and stochastic processes, the fields of an isotropic subspace become commuting random variables, and the description of ideals by subspaces of the one-particle space becomes a description by $σ$-algebras: the ideal generated by the resolvents outside a subspace $Y$ corresponds to the $σ$-algebra of the fields on $Y$, inclusions of the ideals reverse those of the $σ$-algebras, and independence is characterized by conditional expectations.

</details>

Abstract: Higher-order topological insulators and superconductors, under combined point and fundamental symmetries, display topological modes supported by boundaries of higher codimensions, such as a corner mode. Furthermore, we supply a minimal tight-binding model of this phase and design an acoustic crystal whose dynamics reproduce that of the model. The robust properties of the corner mode are investigated by COMSOL simulations.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Data-driven constitutive model discovery is recast as causal discovery of mechanism parent sets using time-unrolled structural causal models. Hard-coding balance laws and constitutive skeletons, candidate graphs score environment--mechanism and state--state edges via weak-form equilibrium gaps evaluated from full-field displacements, avoiding the need to measure stresses or internal variables. An adaptive lasso and a NOTEARS-type constraint ensure graph acyclicity and an explicit evaluation order, treating climate-chamber protocols as interventions. Automatic differentiation yields the score's Gauss--Newton matrix as a local identifiability certificate, driving greedy D-optimal test design. In a synthetic study on a hygro-thermo-viscoelastic damaging plate (8 true edges among 13 candidates), interventional data recover the exact true graph in 7 of 10 runs (0.01px displacement noise) with zero attribution error. Conversely, observational data with a temperature--moisture correlation of $-0.998$ fail entirely because confounded scores are 250--380 times flatter. Observationally discovered models reproduce training climates (3.6% error) but mispredict hot and humid climates by up to 41.5% (16.9% average), whereas interventionally discovered models stay within 3.2%. A correlation sweep shows the certificate successfully predicts attribution errors, though weak-form score noise bias remains a primary limitation. Computed without knowledge of the truth, the D-optimal design selects test sets ranked second or third out of 126 candidates.

</details>


### [93] [Fluctuations of Local Patterns in Non-Periodic Uniquely Ergodic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16685)
*Jacek Miękisz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究正则图顶点上非周期符号构型族的基态构型，提出"严格边界性质"（Strict Boundary Property）以刻画有限模式的有界涨落，并探讨该性质与非周期结构（Robinson铺砌、Thue–Morse、Sturmian系统）在相互作用微扰下稳定性的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非周期但唯一遍历的结构（铺砌、替换系统、Sturmian系统等）是否存在某种"刚性"，使其在相互作用被小扰动时基态构型仍保持稳定，是一个核心问题。作者希望用有限模式涨落的有界性来统一刻画这种稳定性，并解释现有各类非周期系统为何具有（或不具有）该刚性。

Method: 将符号视为粒子，把构型视为正则图顶点上的粒子排布；在作用力（相互作用能）下定义基态构型，即能量极小构型。假定所研究构型支撑唯一遍历测度。引入"严格边界性质"——刻画非周期构型有限模式的有界涨落——并分析该性质与基态在相互作用小扰动下稳定性的蕴含关系；随后以Robinson铺砌、Thue–Morse序列与Sturmian系统作为实例进行检验。

Result: 给出严格边界性质的形式化定义，并论证其与非周期结构稳定性之间的关联；通过Robinson铺砌、Thue–Morse与Sturmian系统的例子展示该性质在具体系统中的表现，说明何种唯一遍历非周期系统具备此类刚性。

Conclusion: 有限模式的有界涨落（严格边界性质）可作为判断唯一遍历非周期结构稳定性的有效判据，为铺砌、替换系统与Sturmian系统等的刚性提供了统一视角，并由此提出若干待解决的开放问题。

Abstract: We study various families of non-periodic configurations of symbols assigned to vertices of regular graphs. We restrict ourselves to such systems where considered configurations support a uniquely ergodic measure. Symbols can be interpreted as particles and we are concerned with configurations which minimize the interaction energy of particles, the so-called ground-state configurations. One of the main questions then is whether ground-state configurations are stable with respect to small perturbations of interactions. Such stability may be interpreted as a sort of rigidity of non-periodic structures such as tilings, substitution systems, Sturmian systems, and other uniquely ergodic non-periodic systems. We introduce a property of bounded fluctuations of finite patterns of non-periodic configurations, the so-called Strict Boundary Property. We discuss how relevant this property is for stability of non-periodic structures. Examples of Robinson's tilings, Thue-Morse, and Sturmian systems are presented. Some open problems are formulated.

</details>


### [94] [Finite Bodies on Norton's Dome: Escape-Time Divergence and a Center-of-Mass Barrier Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16797)
*Guan-Chuan Chen,En-Jui Kuo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Norton穹顶中质点自发离顶的非唯一性问题推广到有限尺寸刚体：证明任何固定正半径球都排除自发滑动，并给出两个充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Norton穹顶表明牛顿初值问题在同一初始状态下可同时容许静止解与延迟脱离解，这种非唯一性长期被视为质点理想化的产物；作者关心当质点被替换为正半径刚球或更一般有限体时，该非唯一性是否仍然存在，从而厘清几何尺寸对自发运动的影响。

Method: 针对广义Norton穹顶族，先对固定正半径球做几何分析排除自发离顶；再推广到一般物体，给出两个充分条件：(i) 质心到顶点距离的几何约束；(ii) 质心与初始接触点竖直对齐时的内部切球条件。论证结合几何估计、能量不等式与Grönwall不等式。

Result: 对任意固定正半径的球，在任何广义Norton穹顶族上均不存在自发离开静止状态的运动；第一条件同时适用于无可释放内能的被动可变形体；两条件均为有限体几何排除自发运动的显式判据。

Conclusion: Norton穹顶的自发运动依赖于质点理想化；一旦引入正尺寸与相应几何条件（质心-顶点距离约束或内部切球条件），非唯一性即被排除，从而明确界定了有限体几何何时排除了质点模型所允许的自发运动。

Abstract: Norton's dome illustrates how a Newtonian initial-value problem can admit both rest and delayed departure from the same initial state. We examine how this nonuniqueness changes when the point particle is replaced by a rigid sphere of positive radius. For a generalized family of Norton's domes, we show that any fixed positive sphere radius prevents spontaneous departure from rest at the apex. We then extend the analysis to more general bodies and establish two sufficient conditions: a geometric constraint on the distance between the center of mass and the apex, and an interior tangent ball condition for rigid bodies whose center of mass is vertically aligned with the initial contact point. Both arguments combine geometric estimates with an energy inequality and Grönwall's inequality. The first condition also applies to passive deformable bodies without releasable internal energy. These results identify explicit conditions under which finite-body geometry excludes the spontaneous motion allowed by Norton's point-particle model.

</details>


### [95] [Concentration of Quasimodes at Spherical Interface between 3D-Topological Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16809)
*Víctor Arnaiz,Éric Vacelet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在半经典Dirac方程连续模型框架下研究两个三维拓扑绝缘体球形界面上局域化的边缘态，刻画界面处拟模的半经典测度的集中性与不变性，并构造沿球面单条周期测地线集中的显式拟模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维拓扑绝缘体之间的界面存在局域边缘态，其半经典（高能/小参数）极限下的相空间分布规律缺乏系统描述，需要从连续Dirac模型角度给出测度层面的定量刻画。

Method: 采用半经典Dirac方程连续模型；利用与界面相切的半经典Dirac算子的动力学与谱性质，并结合横变量方向能量的Landau能级分层（分层结构）进行分析；进而构造显式拟模。

Result: 给出界面局域拟模所关联半经典测度的若干集中性与不变性刻画；构造出满足部分上述性质、并集中于球面某条特定周期测地线上的显式拟模。

Conclusion: 界面边缘态的半经典行为可由切向Dirac动力学与横向量子化的Landau分层共同决定，半经典测度受不变性约束；周期测地线可作为拟模的集中轨道，为边缘态相空间结构的构造性理解提供依据。

Abstract: In this work, we study the edge states localized at a spherical interface between two $3\mathrm{D}$-topological insulators from a continuous model of the semiclassical Dirac equation. Using the dynamical and spectral properties of the semiclassical Dirac operator tangent to the interface and the Landau level stratification of the energy in the transverse variable, we describe several concentration and invariance properties of the semiclassical measures associated with quasimodes of the system localized at the interface. Moreover, we construct explicit quasimodes which fulfill some of these properties and concentrate on some particular single periodic geodesics of the sphere.

</details>


### [96] [Spin-Boson Hamiltonians as Generators of Spin-Dressed Bogoliubov Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17025)
*Nathan Metraud*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: The paper extends Bach–Lieb–Solovej's dynamical definition of quadratic Hamiltonians (generators of unitary groups implementing Bogoliubov transformations) to generalized spin–boson Hamiltonians. Since no analogue of such a group exists for several spin levels, "spin-dressed implementations" are introduced, characterized by a one-particle Hamiltonian Υ and a coupling map; these exactly reproduce generalized spin–boson Hamiltonians at the finest regularity scale, and cover renormalized two-level systems with UV-singular form factors at the roughest scale.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quadratic Hamiltonians admit a clean dynamical characterization as generators of unitary groups implementing one-parameter Bogoliubov transformations (Bach–Lieb–Solovej). One would like an analogous intrinsically dynamical definition for spin–boson Hamiltonians, but the naive group-theoretic analogue breaks down as soon as several spin levels are present, so a weaker notion is needed.

Method: Review of Guichardet–Dereziński results for a single spin level: van Hove Hamiltonians (including renormalized ones) are exactly generators of affine Bogoliubov dynamics not mixing creation and annihilation operators. For several spin levels the authors introduce spin-dressed implementations: the free field flow is implemented up to bounded operator-valued displacements vanishing at a rate measured by a regularity scale, whose derivative at time zero is a spin observable (the coupling map). The one-particle Hamiltonian Υ and the coupling map are determined by the Hamiltonian and the field satisfies a quantum Langevin equation.

Result: In Fock space, at the finest scale with form factors in D(Υ^{-1/2}), spin-dressed implementations coincide exactly with generalized spin–boson Hamiltonians, unique up to the spin Hamiltonian. At the roughest scale, the renormalized spin–boson Hamiltonian of a two-level system with ultraviolet-singular form factor and arbitrary tunnelling is a spin-dressed implementation; the definition alone yields asymptotic Weyl operators on test functions along which the coupling map is integrable, a dense set under standard assumptions.

Conclusion: Spin-dressed implementations provide a unified, purely dynamical framework that interpolates between well-behaved (Fock-space, regular form factor) spin–boson models and singular renormalized ones, replacing the unavailable Bogoliubov-group structure by displacement-type implementers governed by a regularity scale and coupling map.

Abstract: Following Bach, Lieb and Solovej, quadratic Hamiltonians can be defined as generators of unitary groups implementing one-parameter groups of Bogoliubov transformations. We develop an analogous dynamical definition of generalized spin--boson Hamiltonians. For a single spin level, we review results of Guichardet and Dereziński: the van Hove Hamiltonians, including the renormalized ones, are exactly the generators of affine Bogoliubov dynamics that do not mix creation and annihilation operators. For several spin levels, there is no analogue of this group, and we introduce spin-dressed implementations, that is to say the free flow of the fields is implemented up to bounded operator-valued displacements, which vanish at a rate measured by a so-called regularity scale and have a spin observable, the coupling map, as derivative at time zero. The one-particle Hamiltonian $Υ$ and the coupling map are determined by the Hamiltonian, and the field obeys a quantum Langevin equation. Spin-dressed implementations in Fock space, at the finest scale and for form factors in $D(Υ^{-1/2})$, are exactly the generalized spin--boson Hamiltonians, unique up to the spin Hamiltonian. At the roughest scale, the renormalized spin--boson Hamiltonian of a two-level system with ultraviolet-singular form factor and arbitrary tunnelling is a spin-dressed implementation, and the definition alone yields asymptotic Weyl operators on the test functions along which the coupling map is integrable, a dense set under standard assumptions.

</details>


### [97] [Generalized ladder operators and construction of families of superintegrable systems with Painlevé VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17117)
*Ian Marquette,Anthony J. L. Parr*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为分离角向部分由一般 Painlevé VI 超超越函数表述的量子哈密顿量构造广义阶梯算符，得到任意高阶积分与变形 Racah 型多项式代数，并系统分析其特殊解扇区的谱结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tremblay-Turbiner-Winternitz (TTW) 与 Post-Winternitz (PW) 型超可积模型此前多限于特定角向势（如 Pöschl-Teller、三角/双曲型），其高阶积分与代数结构依赖于角向哈密顿量的可解性。作者希望将角向部分推广到一般的 Painlevé VI 超超越函数，从而生成新族超可积哈密顿量，并把三阶、四阶积分分类中的模型作为特例纳入统一框架。

Method: 构造具二次谱变量的二维 Schrödinger 算符的广义阶梯算符；对 Painlevé VI 角向哈密顿量，取 N=√M，验证 [N,J±]=±2kJ±，并由八次范数多项式闭合。将角向平移与振子、Coulomb、自由 Bessel 体系的径向平移算符结合，得到高阶积分与变形 Racah 型多项式代数。对代表性振子扩展显式计算 Casimir 并通过变形振子实现恢复有限维谱；借助 Gromak–Laine–Shimomura 收集的显式特殊解族分析特殊解扇区，区分阶梯范数的形式零点与物理自伴表示。

Result: 得到含 Painlevé VI 势、具任意高阶积分的新族 TTW 型与 PW 型哈密顿量，并复现三阶、四阶积分分类模型为特例。特殊解按少量谱类组织：正则非经典有理例为三角 Pöschl-Teller 系统的有理 Darboux 变形，给出严格等谱的例外 Jacobi 扇区或带孤立角向能级的状态添加表示；奇异分支产生内部逆平方势垒与分裂定义域问题。对一个正则例外扇区与两个状态添加扇区显式给出多项式代数。

Conclusion: Painlevé VI 角向结构可作为生成高阶超可积 TTW/PW 类模型的统一机制，其代数由八次范数多项式闭合的阶梯算符刻画；特殊解扇区的物理谱需按自伴表示而非范数形式零点判定，由此得到的例外 Jacobi 等谱扇区与状态添加表示构成可明确刻画的谱类。

Abstract: We study quantum Hamiltonians whose separated angular component is expressed through a generic Painlevé VI transcendent. We construct generalized ladder operators for one-dimensional Schrödinger operators with a quadratic spectral variable. For the Painlevé VI angular Hamiltonian these operators satisfy $[N,J_\pm]=\pm2kJ_\pm$, where $N=\sqrt M$, and close through an eighth-degree norm polynomial. Combining the angular shifts with the radial shift operators of the oscillator, Coulomb and free Bessel systems yields higher-order integrals and deformed Racah-type polynomial algebras for the two-dimensional superintegrable models. This construction provides new families of Tremblay-Turbiner-Winternitz (TTW)- and Post-Winternitz (PW)-like Hamiltonians with integrals of arbitrarily high order and Painlevé VI in the potential. This framework also recovers models from the classification of third- and fourth-order integrals as particular cases. For a representative oscillator extension we compute the Casimir explicitly and recover the finite-dimensional energy spectrum from a deformed-oscillator realization. We then analyze the special-solution sector using the explicit families collected by Gromak, Laine and Shimomura. The resulting potentials are organized into a small number of spectral classes, and the distinction between formal zeros of the ladder norm and physical self-adjoint representations is made explicit. The regular nonclassical rational examples are rational Darboux deformations of the trigonometric Pöschl--Teller system and lead to strictly isospectral exceptional-Jacobi sectors or to state-adding representations with an isolated angular level. A singular branch produces an interior inverse-square barrier and a split-domain problem. For one regular exceptional sector and two state-adding sectors we display the polynomial algebras.

</details>


### [98] [Existence of a fluid phase for the two-dimensional one-component plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17174)
*Jeanne Boursier,Thomas Leblé*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了二维单组分等离子体在逆温度 β≤2 时关联函数呈指数衰减，且在其涨落受控的点过程类中 Gibbs 态唯一，从而给出无穷体积极限（推广的 Ginibre 过程 Gin_β）的存在性，并验证其截断两点函数在 Debye 长度尺度下的一阶 β 展开符合 Debye–Hückel 近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维单组分等离子体是物理学家预测存在流体相（fluid phase）的典型模型，但该流体相区域在数学上缺乏严格刻画，尤其是 β≤2 范围内关联衰减、Gibbs 态唯一性与无穷体积极限的存在性此前未被证明。

Method: 对对数排斥、均匀中性背景下的点电荷系统，在 β≤2 时通过分析关联函数的衰减行为证明其指数衰减；并在“涨落受控”的点过程类中证明 Gibbs 态唯一。由此构造无穷体积极限，将其定义为推广的 Ginibre 点过程 Gin_β（β=2 时退化为经典 Ginibre 过程）。随后在 Debye 长度尺度上对截断两点函数作 β 的一阶展开，与 Debye–Hückel 线性化理论对比。

Result: 证明 β≤2 时关联函数指数衰减、Gibbs 态唯一；建立无穷体积极限 Gin_β 的存在性；并证明在 Debye 长度尺度下，其截断两点函数在 β 一阶近似下由 Debye–Hückel 近似给出。

Conclusion: 结果从数学上证实了物理学家关于 β≤2 流体相预言的一部分，将 Ginibre 点过程纳入更一般的单参数族 Gin_β，并说明弱耦合极限下 Debye–Hückel 近似的正确性。

Abstract: The two-dimensional one-component plasma is a system of point charges that repel each other logarithmically, in a uniform neutralizing background. When the inverse temperature $β$ is at most $2$, we prove that its correlations decay exponentially, and that its Gibbs state is unique among point processes whose fluctuations are controlled. This constitutes part of the fluid phase predicted by physicists. As a consequence, we obtain the existence of an infinite-volume limit, extending the Ginibre point process, which we call $\mathrm{Gin}_β$. We show that its truncated two-point function, at the scale of the Debye length, is given to first order in $β$ by the Debye-Hückel approximation.

</details>
