Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍Euclid DR1测光掩膜（photometric masks）：按天区tile构建、以Nside=16384（12.88″）的HEALPix全天天图形式提供，覆盖EWS与EDS，用于刻画巡天属性、仪器性能与外部数据质量，并界定有效巡天面积、剔除伪源；EWS与EDS总面积分别为2008.8与74.7 deg²，有效覆盖掩膜保留约82%/81%，EWS深度中位数在IE/YE/JE/HE波段为25.69/24.14/24.30/24.29 AB mag（2″孔径、5σ），深度标准差小于0.2 mag。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid DR1的测光样本选择与宇宙学分析需要准确掌握巡天空间非均匀性：卫星位置角、积分时间、天区拼接与巡天足迹等巡天属性，仪器与外部数据的覆盖、深度、PSF宽度、质量标记、系统效应与天体测量不确定度，以及银河系消光、黄道光等天空发射污染，都会影响测光质量与样本纯度。因此需要一套统一的掩膜产品来量化这些变化、界定有效巡天面积并剔除伪源。

Method: 按每个观测天区（tile）分别构建Euclid测光掩膜，汇总巡天属性、仪器性能与EXT数据质量信息及天空发射污染信息，并生成Nside=16384（nested排序、显式索引）的全天HEALPix地图，对应12.88″分辨率，覆盖Euclid宽巡天（EWS）与深巡天（EDS）；其中有效覆盖掩膜（effective coverage mask）用于定义有效巡天面积并在测光样本选择中排除伪源；同时统计EWS的深度分布（2″孔径、5σ）。

Result: DR1 EWS与EDS总面积估计为2008.8 deg²与74.7 deg²，有效覆盖掩膜分别保留82%与81%的面积。仪器性能与预期一致，仅呈现较小的天空间相对变化；EWS深度标准差低于0.2 AB mag，IE、YE、JE、HE波段深度中位数分别为25.69、24.14、24.30与24.29 AB mag。

Conclusion: Euclid DR1测光掩膜系统刻画了巡天的空间非均匀性，有效覆盖掩膜是界定有效面积与净化样本的关键工具，预期将广泛服务于各类科学应用，尤其是宇宙学分析。

Abstract: This paper describes the Euclid DR1 photometric masks, which trace spatial variations in the survey properties (e.g. satellite position angles, integration time, sky tiling, and survey footprint); in the instrumental performance of Euclid and the external data (EXT) it uses (e.g. survey coverage, depth, PSF width, quality flags, systematic effects and astrometric uncertainties); and in possible contamination from sky emission (e.g. galactic extinction and zodiacal light emission). Among this, the effective coverage mask is particularly important as it defines the effective survey area and can be used to exclude spurious objects when selecting Euclid photometric samples for scientific analyses. The Euclid photometric masks are built for each observed sky region (tile) and are provided as full-sky HEALPix maps (nested ordering and explicit indexing) at Nside = 16 384, equating to a resolution of 12"88, for both the Euclid Wide Survey (EWS) and Euclid Deep Survey (EDS). We estimate the total area of the DR1 EWS (EDS) to be 2008.8 (74.7) deg$^2$ and find that 82 (81)\% of this area is preserved by the effective coverage mask. We observe that Euclid's instrumental properties are consistent with expectations, showing only minor relative variations across the sky. For DR1 EWS the standard deviation of the depth (computed for a 2" aperture and at 5 $σ$) is below 0.2 AB mag, with median values of 25.69, 24.14, 24.3, and 24.29 for the Euclid IE, YE, JE, and HE bands. Finally, we expect these photometric masks to be key to many science applications and in particular for cosmological analyses.

</details>


### [130] [Modelling redshift-space distortions with tracer-centred split densities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17009)
*Aritra K. Gon,Yan-Chuan Cai,Mark Neyrinck,Andrew Repp,Ravi K. Sheth,István Szapudi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于示踪物中心密度PDF的红移空间密度分割（DS）建模框架，解决DS中心在实空间与红移空间不守恒的难题，并论证DS聚类对宇宙学参数的约束优于标准两点统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DS方法在提取宇宙学信息上优于标准两点聚类，但观测位于红移空间，模建困难，核心障碍是DS中心在实空间与红移空间之间可能不守恒。

Method: 采用基于示踪物中心密度PDF（而非以往体积加权密度PDF）进行密度分割；推导红移空间阈值密度对应的线性偏置，将大尺度DS聚类与密度PDF联系起来；识别阈值密度场呈"甜甜圈"状结构（称为Island）；提出类比晕模型的唯象模型，包含线性、非线性与随机项。

Result: 所提唯象模型能准确重现各DS分箱中示踪物的各向异性红移空间聚类；确认DS聚类对宇宙学参数的约束比标准两点统计更紧。

Conclusion: DS分析相对标准两点统计的宇宙学信息增益主要来源于对密度PDF的知识，其中包含平滑尺度以下的小尺度扰动模式。

Abstract: Analysing galaxy clustering in split environmental densities has been demonstrated to offer an advantage over standard two-point clustering for extracting cosmological information. A major challenge to realising this potential with the density-split (DS) technique is that the observations are in redshift space. This complicates its modelling, primarily because the density-split centres may not be conserved between real and redshift space. We develop a modelling framework that splits on the tracer-centred density PDF, rather than the volume-weighted density PDF commonly used in previous studies. We derive the linear bias associated with the threshold density in redshift space, enabling us to connect large-scale DS clustering to the density PDF. We find that the threshold density field consists of doughnut-like structures, which we term `Island(s)'. We propose an empirical model, analogous to the halo model framework, that comprises linear, non-linear, and stochastic terms and accurately reproduces the anisotropic redshift-space clustering of tracers in each DS bin. Using this model, we confirm that DS clustering can provide tighter constraints on cosmological parameters than the standard two-point statistic. Furthermore, we show that the gain in cosmological information from DS analyses relative to standard two-point statistics arises primarily from knowledge of the density PDF, which incorporates small-scale perturbation modes below the smoothing scale.

</details>


### [131] [Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1): The Euclid surveys at DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17040)
*Euclid Collaboration,I. Tereno,J. Dinis,X. Dupac,R. Scaramella,B. Altieri,H. Aussel,I. Baldry,P. Battaglia,G. Buenadicha,C. Burigana,B. Carry,P. Casenove,J. -C. Cuillandre,C. Dominguez-Tagle,A. Franco,B. R. Granett,M. Granvik,A. Gregorio,B. Y. Irureta-Goyena,R. Kohley,K. Kuijken,P. Liebing,E. L. Martin,J. Martin-Fleitas,J. Mendes,L. Miller,A. A. Nucita,R. Nakajima,K. Paterson,V. Pettorino,M. Pontinen,G. D. Racca,E. Romelli,M. Schirmer,M. Schmidt,T. Schrabback,D. Scott,C. Sirignano,R. Tata,T. Trombetti,R. Vavrek,A. Zacchei,M. Zerjal*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍Euclid任务数据发布DR1中各类巡天与天区的状态，包括深场巡天、辅助场、定标场、中深巡天、黄道巡天及独立遗留科学家场，并详述从需求约束到在轨执行的操作流程；DR1中主巡天覆盖2100 deg²，其中1900 deg²已完成高层验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid虽以宽场成像与光谱测绘为主，但任务实际包含多个巡天组件与特殊天区，这些数据分散在不同任务阶段（性能验证期、位相分集定标期、科学运行期），需要系统性说明其实现过程与DR1发布状态，以便用户社区正确理解与使用各数据集。

Method: 论文梳理多类Euclid巡天与天区的组织方式，系统描述巡天实施流程：从需求、约束与限制的确定，到用于航天器操作与科学巡天执行的最终运行产品；并区分性能验证阶段、位相分集定标阶段与科学运行阶段的实施程序。

Result: DR1中Euclid宽场巡天覆盖2100 deg²，分布于两个独立区域；其中1900 deg²的数据已通过更高层级处理验证。其余组件（深场、辅助场、定标场、中深巡天、黄道巡天、独立遗留科学家场）亦给出对应状态与阶段归属。

Conclusion: Euclid DR1不止于宽场巡天，而是多组件巡天体系的首次综合发布；各阶段实施流程与运行产品的规范化描述，为后续数据发布和科学利用提供了基线参考。

Abstract: Euclid is a surveying mission that is progressively imaging and spectroscopically mapping a wide area of extragalactic sky in visible and near infrared bands. The Euclid mission is, however, not restricted to the Euclid Wide Survey, but it includes data from various other survey components. This paper presents the Data Release 1 (DR1) status of the multiple Euclid surveys and fields, including the Euclid Deep Survey, Euclid Auxiliary Fields, calibration fields, the Euclid Medium-Deep Survey, the Euclid Ecliptic Survey, and the Independent Legacy Scientists' field, in addition to the Euclid Wide Survey. Some of these fields were observed during the initial Performance-Verification phase, others during the Phase-Diversity calibration campaign, and the majority during the subsequent science operations phase. This paper describes the different procedures of survey implementation applied in the various phases of the mission, from the set of requirements, constraints, and limitations, to the final operational products used to operate the spacecraft and execute the science surveys. The resulting main component of the Euclid surveys, the Euclid Wide Survey, covers at DR1 an area of 2100$\,{\rm deg}^2$, in two separate regions, of which 1900$\,{\rm deg}^2$ corresponds to data validated in higher-level processing.

</details>


### [132] [On-the-Fly Gaussian Process Reconstruction of the Galaxy Redshift Distribution for 3$\times$2pt Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17085)
*Jip de Buck,Guadalupe Cañas-Herrera,Henk Hoekstra,Angus H. Wright*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 用高斯过程(GP)非参数化重建光度红移分布 n(z)，在类Euclid的3×2pt合成数据上联合采样GP超参与宇宙学参数，发现推断结果对n(z)模型的依赖在 w0waCDM 中显著强于 ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3×2pt分析精度关键取决于 n(z) 的标定，残余不确定性会偏置宇宙学推断；而传统"逐bin平移+展宽"的参数化冗余模型难以覆盖真实 n(z) 的全部可能误差形式，需更灵活的建模方式。

Method: 以高斯过程对 n(z) 做逐bin的非参数、数据驱动重建，取代平移-宽度参数化；在代表 Euclid DR1 的合成 3×2pt 数据矢量上，将GP超参数与宇宙学、冗余参数联合采样，从而把 n(z) 不确定性传播到后验中，并在 ΛCDM 与 w0waCDM 下对比。

Result: 推断参数对所选 n(z) 模型的依赖在 w0waCDM 中明显更强；放开暗能量自由度既放大了给定 n(z) 处理导致的偏置，也改变了数据所偏好的 n(z) 重建——在 ΛCDM 中验证良好的模型不一定在 w0、wa 自由时仍适用。

Conclusion: Stage-IV巡天应针对超出 ΛCDM 的宇宙学单独制定 n(z) 不确定性的边缘化要求，以保证暗能量约束无偏。

Abstract: The joint analysis of weak gravitational lensing, photometric galaxy clustering, and their cross-correlation, the so-called 3$\times$2pt analysis, is one of the primary probes Stage-IV surveys will use to constrain the dark-energy equation of state. Its accuracy depends critically on the calibration of the underlying photometric redshift distributions, $n(z)$, whose residual uncertainties can bias cosmological inference. However, conventional parametric nuisance models may not adequately capture the full range of plausible $n(z)$ uncertainties. We therefore present a cosmological inference framework that instead reconstructs $n(z)$ on the fly using Gaussian Processes (GPs), replacing per-bin shift-and-width parameterisations by a non-parametric, data-driven model. We validate the method on synthetic Euclid-like 3$\times$2pt data vectors representative of Euclid Data Release 1, sampling GP hyperparameters jointly with cosmological and nuisance parameters to reliably propagate $n(z)$ uncertainties into the posterior distributions. We find that the dependence of the inferred parameters on the adopted $n(z)$ model is markedly stronger in $w_0w_a$CDM than in $Λ$CDM. Opening the dark-energy sector both amplifies the biases induced by a given $n(z)$ treatment and changes which $n(z)$ reconstruction the data prefer, so that a model validated in $Λ$CDM does not automatically remain adequate once $w_0$ and $w_a$ are free. Our results thus indicate that Stage-IV surveys should define $n(z)$ uncertainty marginalisation requirements separately in cosmologies beyond $Λ$CDM to ensure unbiased dark-energy constraints.

</details>


### [133] [The 21 cm-Lyman-$α$ forest cross-power spectrum with a minimal effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17177)
*G. Autieri,V. Iršič,M. Viel*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文检验一个仅含树级Kaiser模型加领头阶反项与随机项的"最小有效场论"(mEFT)能否同时对21 cm自功率谱、Lyα自功率谱及其交叉功率谱给出良好拟合，并据此约束中性氢偏置与平均亮温。基于Sherwood 80 h⁻¹Mpc流体模拟（HI涂敷于晕，z=2.4–4.2）并用Zel'dovich控制变量减小样本方差，mEFT在所有五个红移下同时拟合三条谱：联合拟合将HI偏置约束到2.0–2.5%、Lyα密度偏置约束到2.1–2.3%；但当亮温自由时，含交叉谱的拟合偏好比真值高20–30%（1–2σ），需对HI偏置加先验才能恢复真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 21 cm线与Lyα森林强度图探测同一宇宙体积（2≲z≲4），其交叉功率谱对不相关的前景不敏感，且与Lyα自谱结合可分离平均21 cm亮温与中性氢(HI)偏置。然而，两示踪量的EFT模型在一圈阶时携带数十个冗余参数，实用性差。因此需要检验是否可用极简的mEFT（树级Kaiser+领头阶反项+随机项）统一描述三条谱，从而在可控参数数量下实现上述物理分离。

Method: 采用Sherwood 80 h⁻¹Mpc流体动力学模拟，在晕上涂敷HI，覆盖z=2.4–4.2共五个红移；用Zel'dovich控制变量降低样本方差。mEFT取树级Kaiser模型加领头阶反项和随机项（此前已在Lyα自谱上验证）。先分别单独验证各谱，再做共享参数的联合拟合。截断尺度为k_max=0.5、1.2、2.0 h Mpc⁻¹，分别对应21 cm自谱、交叉谱与Lyα自谱，并分别与HI速度弥散和Lyα压致平滑尺度相联系。另分别测试亮温自由、以及加入HI偏置先验的情景。

Result: 在全部五个红移上，mEFT均能同时拟合21 cm自谱、交叉谱和Lyα自谱。联合拟合给出HI偏置精度2.0–2.5%，Lyα密度偏置精度2.1–2.3%。当亮温作为自由参数时，含交叉谱的拟合偏好高于真值20–30%的亮温（1–2σ显著性）；而仅用两条自谱的联合并不对亮温构成约束。对HI偏置施加先验后，可恢复亮温真值。

Conclusion: 参数极少（远少于一圈EFT的数十个冗余参数）的mEFT即可在k_max≈1.2 h Mpc⁻¹量级下联合描述21 cm自谱、Lyα自谱与交叉谱，并以约2%精度约束HI偏置与Lyα密度偏置；截断尺度的选择具有物理依据（HI速度弥散、Lyα压致平滑）。不过，自由亮温下的交叉谱会带来1–2σ的20–30%偏高偏差，单靠自谱无法约束亮温，需引入HI偏置先验，提示实际数据分析中联合约束亮温需谨慎处理系统偏差。

Abstract: Intensity maps of the 21 cm line and the Lyman-$α$ (Ly$α$) forest trace the same cosmic volume at $2\lesssim z\lesssim 4$. Their cross-power spectrum is insensitive to foregrounds that do not correlate between the two probes and, together with the Ly$α$ auto-spectrum, separates the mean 21 cm brightness temperature from the neutral hydrogen (HI) bias. Effective field theory models of each tracer exist, but at one loop they carry tens of nuisance parameters. We test whether a minimal effective field theory (mEFT), consisting of the tree-level Kaiser model with leading-order counterterms and stochastic terms, previously validated for the Ly$α$ auto-spectrum, describes the 21 cm auto-, Ly$α$ auto- and cross-power spectra jointly. We use the Sherwood 80 $h^{-1}$Mpc hydrodynamical simulation with HI painted onto halos at $z=2.4$-$4.2$, and reduce sample variance with Zel'dovich control variates. Each spectrum is validated separately and then in a joint fit with shared parameters. With scale cuts $k_{\rm max}=0.5$, $1.2$ and $2.0\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$ for the 21 cm auto-, cross- and Ly$α$ auto-spectra, the mEFT fits all three spectra at all five redshifts. In the joint fit the mEFT determines the HI bias to 2.0-2.5% and the Ly$α$ density bias to 2.1-2.3%. The scale cuts follow the HI velocity dispersion and the Ly$α$ pressure-smoothing scale. When the brightness temperature is left free, the fits that include the cross-spectrum prefer values 20-30% above the true one, at 1-2$σ$, while the two auto-spectra together do not constrain it. A prior on the HI bias recovers the true value of the brightness temperature.

</details>


### [134] [Parallel alignment of radio AGN jets and large cosmic web structures in the direction of the Shapley supercluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17202)
*I. Baronchelli,T. Venturi,V. Casasola,P. Merluzzi,C. Burigana,G. Busarello,M. Bonato,O. Smirnov,T. Ronconi,M. Massardi,E. Liuzzo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该研究在Shapley超星系团附近发现28个射电AGN喷流存在与前景X射线纤维状结构一致的大尺度排列（总显著性4.5σ），且可能沿视线方向延伸至更大宇宙尺度，暗示宇宙网结构对黑洞自旋轴的持续影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究射电活动星系核（AGN）喷流在Shapley超星系团（SSC）区域是否存在大尺度排列，以及这种排列是否与已识别的宇宙网结构相关联。若存在，将反映大尺度结构对超大质量黑洞自旋轴的潜在影响，并为精确宇宙学测量引入需考虑的系统效应。

Method: 分析28个射电AGN，按位于连接SSC核心星系团的弥散X射线纤维结构南北两侧、天区覆盖相当的子样本划分为两组；测量平均喷流位置角（PA），并结合光谱红移评估排列沿视线的持续性。

Result: 探测到射电AGN喷流与前景X射线纤维（取向PA≈115°±10°）存在显著大尺度排列：北组PA≈117°、南组PA≈129°均与之一致，总体统计显著性4.5σ（上下界6.1σ、3.8σ）。投影排列高度显著，但光谱覆盖稀疏（仅7个源，0.048≲z≲0.55），提示空间相干性可能超出SSC（z≈0.048）范围，沿视线方向在更大宇宙尺度上存在相关性。

Conclusion: 观测到的排列无法仅用局部动力学过程解释，可能反映宇宙网大尺度结构或物质坍缩优势方向对超大质量黑洞自旋轴的持续影响，体现AGN喷流形态与暗物质分布之间的联系；此类排列是Euclid等巡天开展精确宇宙学必须考虑的重要系统效应。

Abstract: Aims. We investigated the potential large-scale alignment of radio active galactic nuclei (AGN) jets in the proximity of the Shapley Supercluster (SSC) area and its correlation with the identified cosmic web structures. Methods. We analyzed 28 radio AGNs divided into two populations with comparable sky coverage north and south of a diffuse X-ray filamentary structure connecting clusters in the SSC core. We measured the average jet position angles (PAs) and combined them with spectroscopic redshifts to assess the persistence of the alignment along the line of sight. Results. We detected a significant large-scale alignment of the radio AGN jets with the foreground X-ray filament (oriented at PA $\sim$ 115$^\circ$ $\pm$ 10$^\circ$). Both the northern (PA $\sim$ 117$^\circ$) and southern (PA $\sim$ 129$^\circ$) groups align with this axis, yielding an overall statistical significance of 4.5 $σ$ (upper and lower bounds: 6.1$σ$, 3.8 $σ$). Although the projected alignment is highly significant, sparse spectroscopic coverage (seven sources spanning 0.048 $\lesssim$ z $\lesssim$ 0.55) suggests that this spatial coherence may extend beyond the SSC (z $\sim$ 0.048), hinting at a line-of-sight correlation across significantly larger cosmic scales. Conclusions. The observed alignment cannot be explained by localized dynamical processes alone. These findings may indicate a persistent influence of the large-scale structure of the cosmic web, or preferred directions of matter collapse, on supermassive black hole spin axes, highlighting a connection between AGN jet morphology and the underlying dark matter distribution. Such alignments represent an important systematic that must be accounted for in precision cosmology with surveys such as Euclid.

</details>


### [135] [Shedding light on modified recombination scenarios with Rayleigh scattering of the CMB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17209)
*Debabrata Adak,Elena Vanetti,R. T. Génova-Santos,Jens Chluba,Carlos Hernández-Monteagudo,J. A. Rubiño-Martín*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究宇宙微波背景光子与中性氢、氦的瑞利散射（RS）信号对复合历史及变常数（电子质量、精细结构常数）的探测能力，用多频Fisher分析预报PICO与LiteBIRD+Planck的参数约束与偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RS在末次散射面期间及稍后引入近似正比于ν^4的频变贡献，主要位于200–800 GHz，可被未来CMB实验探测；由于其频变特性对宇宙学复合过程的电离历史敏感，可作为复合历史的额外探针，从而检验为缓解哈勃张力而提出的变电子质量m_e或变精细结构常数α等修正复合情景。

Method: 构建RS与主CMB信号在温度与极化上的关联；采用含真实噪声与前景的多频Fisher分析，对PICO及LiteBIRD+Planck进行参数约束与偏差预报。

Result: 对PICO，加入RS使ΛCDM宇宙学参数约束改善≲7%，但在若干修正复合情景下对H0、m_e、α、Ω_b h^2、Ω_c h^2的约束可改善约31%；对LiteBIRD+Planck，多数参数改善不足2%。忽略RS会使PICO参数偏差达约1σ，LiteBIRD+Planck则小于1σ。

Conclusion: RS对未来CMB数据分析不可忽略，必须纳入建模；其对修正复合历史的检验价值最为突出。

Abstract: Rayleigh scattering (RS) of cosmic microwave background (CMB) photons by neutral hydrogen and helium, during and shortly after the last scattering surface, adds a frequency-dependent contribution to the CMB anisotropies that scales approximately as $ν^4$ at leading order. The RS signal is most relevant at $200\,{\rm GHz}\lesssimν\lesssim800\,{\rm GHz}$ and could be detected using future CMB experiments. Because RS acts in a frequency-dependent manner which is sensitive to the ionisation history of the cosmological recombination process, it provides an additional probe of the recombination history. This makes it a natural test of modified recombination scenarios, such as variable electron mass $m_{\rm e}$ or fine-structure constant $α$, which have been proposed to ease the Hubble tension. The correlation between the RS and primary CMB signal, both in temperature and polarisation, further enhances its usefulness for constraining cosmological parameters. Using a multi-frequency Fisher analysis with realistic noise and foregrounds, we forecast parameter constraints and biases for PICO and LiteBIRD + Planck. For PICO, adding RS improves the $Λ$CDM constraints on cosmological parameters by $\lesssim7\%$, but improves the constraints on some parameters ($H_0$, $m_{\rm e}$, $α$, $Ω_{\rm b}h^2$, $Ω_{\rm c}h^2$) by $\simeq31\%$ for some modified scenarios in the recombination history. However, the improvement for LiteBIRD + Planck is limited to less than $2\%$ for most of the parameters. Neglecting RS biases the cosmological parameters by up to $\simeq1σ$ for PICO and below $1σ$ for LiteBIRD + Planck. Therefore RS must be modelled in the analyses of future CMB data.

</details>


### [136] [Characterising a population of fast-declining SNe Ia in galaxy cluster cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17242)
*A. Eranhalodi,J. Nordin,M. Kowalski,U. Burgaz,F. Fischer,M. Ginolin,Y. -L. Kim,T. E. Müller-Bravo,J. Sollerman,A. Townsend,A. Drake,M. Graham,D. Hale,J. C. Jaimes,R. R. Laher,J. Purdum*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 利用ZTF档案数据在星系团核心中识别出迄今最大的光变曲线样本，发现核心区SN Ia显著偏向快衰减（低stretch）且与场样本在~4σ水平上不同，支持前身星年龄驱动stretch多样性的双族群模型，并暗示亚Chandrasekhar质量双简并系统为主要前身星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团核心具有老年星族与受抑制的恒星形成，是研究SN Ia环境依赖的理想场所。SN Ia由光变曲线stretch参数化的多样性究竟源于不同爆发机制还是不同初始条件（二者均与前身星年龄密切相关）尚不清楚。

Method: 使用AMPEL平台从89,025个与星系团目录空间重合的ZTF暂现源中筛选出4,969个可能的SN Ia候选体，并分为三组：核心区（r≤0.5R500）、外核区（0.5R500<r≤2R500）和非星系团参考样本；比较其光变曲线stretch与颜色分布，并用AIC检验单峰/双峰分布偏好。

Result: 给出迄今最大的测光识别星系团SN Ia样本，其中核心区含242个SN Ia。核心区快衰减族群与外核区及场样本在~4σ水平上存在统计差异；双峰分布相对单峰分布仅有边际偏好（各目录ΔAIC=1.53和2.3），表明核心区几乎全部为快衰减SN Ia；颜色分布无显著环境差异。

Conclusion: 通过剔除prompt SN Ia族群，星系团核心可直接探测delayed SN Ia前身星通道。观测支持前身星年龄驱动stretch多样性的双族群模型，核心区低stretch值暗示其前身星可能为亚Chandrasekhar质量双简并系统。

Abstract: Galaxy cluster cores, characterised by old stellar population and suppressed star-formation are ideal for studying the environmental dependence of Type Ia supernovae (SNe Ia). It is unclear whether the diversity of SNe Ia, parametrised by light curve stretch, stems from distinct explosion mechanisms or varying initial conditions, both closely tied to progenitor age. We analyse the light curve stretch and colour distribution of photometrically identified SNe Ia within the galaxy clusters cores, utilising the Zwicky Transient Facility (ZTF) archival data. Using the AMPEL platform, we filter 89,025 ZTF transients spatially coincident with our galaxy cluster catalogues to identify 4,969 likely SN Ia candidates. We split this sample into three populations: core cluster SNe Ia $r \le 0.5R_{500}$, outer-core cluster SNe Ia $0.5R_{500}< r\le 2R_{500}$, and a reference population of non-cluster SN Ia. We present the largest sample of photometrically identified cluster SNe Ia to date, notably isolating a population of 242 SNe Ia within innermost cluster cores $r \le 0.5R_{500}$. This fast-declining SN Ia population is statistically different from the outer-core cluster and field SN Ia population at $\sim4σ$ level. A marginal statistical preference for a bimodal distribution over a unimodal one $ΔAIC$ of 1.53 and 2.3 for each catalogue, indicate cluster cores almost entirely host fast-declining SNe Ia. Conversely, the colour distribution show no significant environmental variation. By filtering out the prompt SN Ia population, cluster cores provide an environment to directly probe the delayed SN Ia progenitor channel. Our observations support a two-population model where progenitor age drives the light curve stretch diversity. The low-stretch values observed in core cluster SNe Ia suggest sub-Chandrasekhar mass double degenerate systems as their likely progenitors.

</details>


### [137] [Simulation-Based Priors and BAO Shifts from the Lyman-$α$ Forest at the Field Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17299)
*Divij Sharma,Roger de Belsunce,Mikhail M. Ivanov*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于三阶（立方）大尺度结构微扰理论（有效场论）的莱曼-α森林通量场解析前向模型，在CAMELS与Sherwood流体模拟上验证其将三维通量功率谱拟合精度提升至约5%（k≃1–1.5 h Mpc⁻¹），较此前二阶模型改进最高达40%，并据此测量偏置参数、预测BAO非线性位移、构建DESI先验及协方差估计基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有莱曼-α森林前向模型多为二阶微扰理论，在高波数处精度不足，难以满足DESI等新一代巡天的精确宇宙学需求；同时缺乏对非线性导致的BAO位移的解析刻画以及可靠的协方差矩阵估计手段。

Method: 采用立方阶大尺度结构微扰理论（有效场论）构建通量场解析前向模型；在z=2–4范围内两组流体动力学模拟（小体积、宽参数覆盖的CAMELS套件与大体积、高分辨率的Sherwood模拟）上进行功率谱层面验证；利用场级方法中宇宙方差抵消，对CAMELS进行莱曼-α森林偏置参数的精密测量；基于测量结果解析预测BAO标度参数的非线性位移，并构建模拟先验及包含连通四点函数（trispectrum）的协方差估计框架。

Result: 两组模拟在功率谱层面定性一致；立方模型在k≃1–1.5 h Mpc⁻¹（依红移而定）范围内以约5%精度重现三维通量功率谱，较先前二阶前向模型改进最高40%；BAO标度参数在径向与横向均呈亚百分级位移，且明显依赖宇宙学参数与红移；模型还能刻画森林的连通四点函数（trispectrum）。

Conclusion: 立方微扰前向模型显著提升了莱曼-α森林聚类的解析建模精度，可为DESI（及DESI-II）宇宙学分析提供场级拟合导出的模拟先验和更精确的协方差矩阵估计，并为BAO非线性位移的系统误差修正提供解析工具。

Abstract: We present an analytic forward model for the Lyman-alpha (Ly-alpha) forest flux field based on cubic-order large-scale structure perturbation theory (effective field theory) calculations. We validate our cubic model on two sets of hydrodynamic simulations spanning $2 \leq z \leq 4$: the small-volume CAMELS suite, which spans a wide range of cosmological and astrophysical parameters, and the large-volume, high-resolution Sherwood simulations. We find qualitatively similar results for both at the power-spectrum level. The perturbative model reproduces the three-dimensional flux power spectrum of the simulations to $\sim 5\%$ down to $k \simeq 1$-$1.5\,h\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$, depending on the redshift. This improves compared to previous quadratic perturbative forward models by up to 40%. Exploiting cosmic-variance cancellation in the field-level approach, we carry out precision measurements of the Lyman-alpha forest bias parameters from the CAMELS simulation suite. We use these measurements to analytically predict the shifts in the Baryon Acoustic Oscillations (BAO) scaling parameters induced by nonlinearities in the three-dimensional Lyman-alpha forest clustering as a function of cosmological parameters. We find sub-percent shifts in both the radial and transverse directions with a noticeable dependence on cosmology and redshift. Finally, from the field-level fits we construct simulation-based priors that can be used for cosmological analyses of the Lyman-alpha forest with the currently observing Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI). In addition, our cubic model captures the connected four-point function (trispectrum) of the forest, which opens up novel opportunities for accurate covariance matrix estimation for DESI and its successor, DESI-II.

</details>
